作者：Sameer Pandey。Preprints，2026-10-30

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory、perovskite、active learning。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

金属卤化物和无铅双钙钛矿是下一代光伏发电的有前途的材料。但驾驭其巨大的组成空间并同时控制长期稳定性和非辐射复合是一个挑战。这篇综述提供了跨尺度计算范式的关键综合，该范式汇集了密度泛函理论 (DFT)、机器学习 (ML) 和 SCAPS-1D 等宏观设备模拟。我们讨论如何将第一原理原子参数（例如能带边缘对齐、介电常数和有效质量）转换为有意义的器件参数，而无需物理过度简化。这项工作并不将参数传递视为“即插即用”过程，而是解释了必须在微观能量学和宏观传输方程之间建立的桥接机制。我们严格评估功能依赖性、主动学习效率、设备级限制以及与离子迁移和瞬态动力学相关的不确定性。这篇综述为基于物理的高通量计算发现稳健的钙钛矿太阳能电池提供了可行的路线图。

English Abstract

Metal halide and lead-free double perovskites are promising materials for next-generation photovoltaics. But it is a challenge to navigate their huge compositional space and to simultaneously control long-term stability and non-radiative recombination. This review provides a critical synthesis of the cross-scale computational paradigm that brings together Density Functional Theory (DFT), machine learning (ML) and macroscopic device simulations like SCAPS-1D. We discuss how to transform first-principles atomistic parameters such as band edge alignments, dielectric constants and effective masses into meaningful device parameters without physical oversimplification. This work does not see parameter transfer as a "plug-and-play" process, but rather explains the bridging mechanisms that must be developed between microscopic energetics and macroscopic transport equations. We critically assess functional dependencies, active learning efficiencies, device-level limitations, and uncertainties related to ion migration and transient dynamics. This review provides actionable roadmap towards high-throughput physics-informed computational discovery of robust perovskite solar cells.

通过不确定性感知主动学习实现自主原子连续耦合#

原题：Autonomous atomistic-continuum coupling through uncertainty-aware active learning
作者：Daniel Abdulah、Hariswaran Sitaraman、Ross E. Larsen、Kevin Patrick Griffin、Grace Wei、Janelle Domantay 等 8 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-27

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：我们开发了一种不确定性感知代理建模方法，用于将原子动力学蒙特卡罗（KMC）表面化学求解器与跨不同物理长度和时间尺度的连续多物种流体动力学求解器耦合。替代模型预测六种物质的边界质量通量及其不确定性，连续介质求解器会对其进行监控，以便在需要新的训练数据时自动触发额外的 KMC 模拟。这可以实现高效的多尺度耦合并保持用户指定的精度水平。我们演示了反应性卤化物-钙钛矿气相沉积方法，该方法涉及多物质蒸气射流撞击反应性基材。 KMC 求解器使用我们…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：active learning、perovskite。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们开发了一种不确定性感知代理建模方法，用于将原子动力学蒙特卡罗（KMC）表面化学求解器与跨不同物理长度和时间尺度的连续多物种流体动力学求解器耦合。替代模型预测六种物质的边界质量通量及其不确定性，连续介质求解器会对其进行监控，以便在需要新的训练数据时自动触发额外的 KMC 模拟。这可以实现高效的多尺度耦合并保持用户指定的精度水平。我们演示了反应性卤化物-钙钛矿气相沉积方法，该方法涉及多物质蒸气射流撞击反应性基材。 KMC 求解器使用我们在这项工作中构建的 DFT 校准能量学模型，并提供六种物质的净沉积和解吸速率。在保守的不确定性阈值下，替代耦合方法再现了直接耦合方法的累积质量通量，误差通常在 10-20% 之内，同时捕获了净沉积对物质分压的非单调依赖性。重要的是，结果表明，不需要明确表示详细的晶体结构来预测原子解算器和连续解算器之间交换的量；物种分辨的质量通量提供了原子响应的足够低维的表示。对于这个精度目标，自主在线学习方法相对于直接多尺度耦合将所需的原子模拟数量减少了两到三个数量级。

English Abstract

We develop an uncertainty-aware surrogate modeling approach for coupling an atomistic kinetic Monte Carlo (KMC) surface-chemistry solver with a continuum multi-species fluid dynamics solver across disparate physical length and time scales. The surrogate model predicts boundary mass fluxes for six species and their uncertainties, which the continuum solver monitors to automatically trigger additional KMC simulations when new training data are needed. This enables efficient multiscale coupling and maintains a user-specified level of accuracy. We demonstrate the approach for reactive halide-perovskite vapor deposition, which involves a multi-species vapor jet impinging on a reactive substrate. The KMC solver uses a DFT-calibrated energetics model that we construct as part of this work and provides net deposition and desorption rates for six species. With a conservative uncertainty threshold, the surrogate-coupled approach reproduces cumulative mass fluxes of the directly coupled approach with errors typically within 10-20%, while capturing the non-monotonic dependence of net deposition on species partial pressure. Importantly, the results show that detailed crystal structure need not be explicitly represented to predict quantities exchanged between atomistic and continuum solvers; species-resolved mass fluxes provide a sufficiently low-dimensional representation of the atomistic response. For this accuracy target, the autonomous online-learning approach reduces the number of required atomistic simulations by two to three orders of magnitude relative to direct multiscale coupling.

异构系统的机器学习哈密顿量极化#

原题：Machine Learning Hamiltonians with Polarizations for Heterogeneous Systems
作者：Jia-Yu Zhou、Wentao Zhang、Mouyi Weng、Zhao-Yi Yan、Lin-Wang Wang。arXiv · physics，2026-10-28
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配6

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：机器学习哈密顿量使用局部原子结构来确定局部哈密顿矩阵元素，而无需进行自洽计算。它们已被用于研究大型系统并对其电子和光学特性进行高通量筛选。哈密顿量的预测本质上是电子静电势能的预测；然而，对于异质系统，这种势能通常由远处的特征决定，例如界面或表面偶极子，甚至纳米器件中的施加电压。因此，它不能仅由局部原子结构决定。从这个意义上说，需要使用本征原子偶极子、施加的外部势能和极化率自洽地求解电子静电势能。幸运的是，固有偶极子和极化率是局部属性，而…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习哈密顿量使用局部原子结构来确定局部哈密顿矩阵元素，而无需进行自洽计算。它们已被用于研究大型系统并对其电子和光学特性进行高通量筛选。哈密顿量的预测本质上是电子静电势能的预测；然而，对于异质系统，这种势能通常由远处的特征决定，例如界面或表面偶极子，甚至纳米器件中的施加电压。因此，它不能仅由局部原子结构决定。从这个意义上说，需要使用本征原子偶极子、施加的外部势能和极化率自洽地求解电子静电势能。幸运的是，固有偶极子和极化率是局部属性，而施加的势能是规定的输入。在这项工作中，我们提出了 DeepH-Polar，这是一种物理信息机器学习哈密顿模型，它明确预测本征原子偶极子和极化张量，并使用它们自洽地求解电子静电势能。极性 GaN 系统和晶体-Si/非晶-SiO2 界面的基准测试表明，哈密顿量精度得到了提高，厚度相关静电变化的重现性得到了改善，并且没有极化的模型无法捕获空间不均匀场感应屏蔽。我们的模型还允许我们研究纳米级电子设备。通过迭代求解哈密顿本征问题并自洽更新自由载流子占据和静电势，我们模拟了 8,194 个原子的门控半导体器件。

English Abstract

Machine learning Hamiltonians use local atomic structures to determine local Hamiltonian matrix elements without carrying out self-consistent calculations. They have been used to study large systems and to carry out high-throughput screening for their electronic and optical properties. The prediction of the Hamiltonian is essentially the prediction of the electron electrostatic potential energy; however, for heterogeneous systems, this potential energy is often determined by faraway features, such as interface or surface dipoles, or even an applied voltage in a nanodevice. Thus, it cannot be determined by local atomic structures alone. In this sense, one needs to solve the electron electrostatic potential energy self-consistently using intrinsic atomic dipoles, the applied external potential energy, and polarizabilities. Fortunately, the intrinsic dipoles and polarizabilities are local properties, whereas the applied potential energy is a prescribed input. In this work, we propose DeepH-Polar, a physically informed machine learning Hamiltonian model that explicitly predicts intrinsic atomic dipoles and polarizability tensors and uses them to solve the electron electrostatic potential energy self-consistently. Benchmarks on polar GaN systems and crystalline-Si/amorphous-SiO₂ interfaces demonstrate improved Hamiltonian accuracy and reproduction of thickness-dependent electrostatic variations, as well as spatially nonuniform field-induced screening that models without polarization fail to capture. Our model also allows us to study nanoscale electronic devices. By iteratively solving the Hamiltonian eigenproblem and self-consistently updating free-carrier occupations and the electrostatic potential, we simulate an 8,194-atom gated semiconductor device.

07

Walshness：量子态的内在神经网络可表示性度量#

原题：Walshness: an intrinsic neural-network representability metric for quantum states
作者：Nisarga Paul、Kehuang Chen、Jessica K. Jiang、Haimeng Zhao、Di Luo。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-28
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配3

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：神经量子态（NQS）已经成为量子态的强大表示，在量子多体物理领域的应用迅速扩展。然而，我们对神经网络何时能够有效表示物理量子态的理解仍然有限，部分原因是 NQS 的非线性参数化、量子态复杂的符号结构以及对基选择的敏感依赖性。我们引入了 Walshness，一种量子态的复杂性度量，用于量化它是否接受紧凑的神经网络表示。我们严格证明，对于各种 NQS 架构，如果通用量子多体态具有有界 Walshness，则它承认有效的 NQS 表示，反之亦…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

神经量子态（NQS）已经成为量子态的强大表示，在量子多体物理领域的应用迅速扩展。然而，我们对神经网络何时能够有效表示物理量子态的理解仍然有限，部分原因是 NQS 的非线性参数化、量子态复杂的符号结构以及对基选择的敏感依赖性。我们引入了 Walshness，一种量子态的复杂性度量，用于量化它是否接受紧凑的神经网络表示。我们严格证明，对于各种 NQS 架构，如果通用量子多体态具有有界 Walshness，则它承认有效的 NQS 表示，反之亦然。我们提供了一种通过最小化相应经典自旋模型的能量来找到最佳基础的算法。这使我们能够凭经验提高各种物理量子态的可学习性，包括横向场伊辛模型和混合场环面码的基态，通常可以将不忠实性降低几个数量级，并概括出受挫反铁磁体中著名的马歇尔符号规则给出的基础选择。我们的结果提供了量子态的内在复杂性度量，并与其神经网络可表示性有可证明的联系。

English Abstract

Neural quantum states (NQS) have emerged as powerful representations of quantum states with rapidly expanding applications across quantum many-body physics. Yet our understanding of when neural networks can efficiently represent physical quantum states remains limited, in part due to the nonlinear parameterization of NQS, the intricate sign structure of quantum states, and the sensitive dependence on basis choice. We introduce Walshness, a complexity metric of a quantum state which quantifies whether it admits a compact neural-network representation. We rigorously prove that a general quantum many-body state admits an efficient NQS representation if it has bounded Walshness, and vice versa, for various NQS architectures. We provide an algorithm for finding the optimal basis by minimizing the energy of a corresponding classical spin model. This allows us to empirically improve the learnability of various physical quantum states, including ground states of the transverse-field Ising model and mixed-field toric code, often reducing infidelity by orders of magnitude, and generalizes the basis choice given by the well-known Marshall sign rule in frustrated antiferromagnets. Our results provide an intrinsic complexity metric for quantum states with provable connections to their neural-network representability.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

分数量子霍尔系统具有强相关的拓扑相，在拓扑量子计算中具有潜在的应用，但识别其微观基态仍然具有挑战性，因为竞争相通常几乎是简并的，并且朗道级混合很难处理。在这里，我们引入了一种对称适应的神经网络波函数，它描述阿贝尔和非阿贝尔状态，同时结合所有朗道能级。该方法准确地捕获了复合费米子液体和低填充下维格纳结晶的出现。当第一个朗道能级被部分填充时，有限尺寸的重叠和纠缠谱提供了与 ν=4/3 和 7/5 处的 bar2k1k⁺1 型部分子相一致的证据，而 ν=3/2 态则表现出类反普法夫纠缠结构。这些结果表明，神经网络波函数为研究分数量子霍尔系统中的竞争拓扑序提供了一个灵活的框架。

English Abstract

Fractional quantum Hall systems host strongly correlated topological phases with potential applications in topological quantum computation, yet identifying their microscopic ground states remains challenging because competing phases are often nearly degenerate and Landau-level mixing is difficult to treat. Here, we introduce a symmetry-adapted neural-network wavefunction that describes Abelian and non-Abelian states while incorporating all Landau levels. The method accurately captures composite-fermion liquids and the emergence of Wigner crystallization at low filling. When the first Landau level is partially filled, finite-size overlaps and entanglement spectra provide evidence consistent with bar2k1k⁺¹-type parton phases at ν=4/3 and 7/5, while the ν=3/2 state exhibits anti-Pfaffian-like entanglement structure. These results demonstrate that neural-network wavefunctions offer a flexible framework for studying competing topological orders in fractional quantum Hall systems.

二维磁化等离子体湍流的生成基础模型#

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们提出了一项原理验证研究，展示了自组织磁化等离子体湍流的条件生成基础模型。该模型建立在采用谱分解策略的多通道流量匹配框架之上，并针对从流体动力学到绝热参数范围内的二维修正长谷川-若谷 (MHW) 湍流的直接数值模拟进行训练。无需重新训练，这个单一模型成功地执行了三个不同的下游任务：（i）无条件生成统计上忠实的准稳态快照，恢复方向能谱和振幅统计数据，从中直接推断出统计传输量，包括湍流通量和有效扩散率及其不确定性； (ii) 4×和8×下采样观测的超分辨率，以重建细尺度湍流和大尺度带状结构； (iii) 根据稀疏空间观测进行全场修复，覆盖面积小至 sim 10%。生成的场忠实地再现了依赖于状态的湍流与纬向流状态和能量分配。在极端稀疏的情况下，长相关纬向流仅由少数观测结果固定，而剩余不确定性集中在小尺度湍流波动中。这些结果建立了流匹配生成模型作为状态生成、统计推断和稀疏数据重建的强大、统一的替代物，为 3D 等离子体湍流和实验应用提供了可扩展的基线。

English Abstract

We present a proof-of-principle study demonstrating a conditional generative foundation model for self-organizing magnetized plasma turbulence. Built on a multichannel flow-matching framework with a spectral decomposition strategy, the model is trained on direct numerical simulations of 2D modified Hasegawa-Wakatani (MHW) turbulence across the hydrodynamic to adiabatic parameter range. Without retraining, this single model successfully performs three distinct downstream tasks: (i) unconditional generation of statistically faithful quasi-steady-state snapshots that recover the directional energy spectra and amplitude statistics, from which statistical transport quantities, including turbulent flux and effective diffusivity, alongside their uncertainties, are inferred directly; (ii) super-resolution from 4× and 8× downsampled observations to reconstruct fine-scale turbulence and large-scale zonal structures; and (iii) full-field inpainting from sparse spatial observations covering as little as sim 10% of the domain. The generated fields faithfully reproduce regime-dependent turbulence vs. zonal-flow states and energy partitioning. Under extreme sparsity the long-correlation zonal flows are pinned by only a handful of observations, while the residual uncertainty concentrates in the small-scale turbulent fluctuations. These results establish flow-matching generative models as powerful, unified surrogates for state generation, statistical inference, and sparse-data reconstruction, providing a scalable baseline for 3D plasma turbulence and experimental applications.

中文摘要

我们提出了一种用于生成任意三轴磁波形的系统，其频谱内容从直流到数十kHz，这是量子控制和自旋操纵的关键能力。为了补偿放大器线圈动态，我们采用数据驱动的方法来确定数值补偿模型。该方法使用根据要生成的特定波形进行校准的有限脉冲响应 (FIR) 滤波器来参数化系统响应。通过对已识别模型进行频域反演而设计的驱动信号的应用能够合成复杂的场序列，并在静态和单频或多频时间段之间进行急剧的过渡。实验结果验证了该工作的波形保真度和瞬态性能，从而展示了该方法的精度和可行性。

English Abstract

We present a system for generating arbitrary, triaxial magnetic waveforms with a spectral content spanning from DC to tens of kHz, a critical capability for quantum control and spin manipulation. To compensate for amplifier-coil dynamics, we implement a data-driven approach to identify a numerical compensation model. The method parametrizes the system response using a Finite Impulse Response (FIR) filter calibrated on the specific waveform to be generated. The application of a driving signal designed via frequency-domain inversion of the identified model enables the synthesis of complex field sequences with sharp transitions between static and single- or multi-frequency temporal segments. The work is validated with experimental results demonstrating waveform fidelity and transient performance, thereby showcasing the precision and feasibility of the method.

从 MS/MS 证据到自适应光谱表征：候选分子结构引导的强化学习#

原题：From MS/MS evidence to adaptive spectroscopic characterization: Reinforcement learning guided by candidate molecular structures

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

串联质谱 (MS/MS) 中的小分子鉴定可能需要补充测量。在此，我们提出了一种基于结构的深度 Q 网络 (SI-DQN)，它使用现有的 MS/MS 证据来决定是否需要进一步表征、请求哪种测量以及何时停止。该政策将光谱表示和候选排名与候选化学特征和结构差异的分数加权描述符结合起来。该政策通过来自多模态光谱数据集的模拟 MS/MS、红外 (IR) 和核磁共振 (NMR) 光谱对 79,462 个保留样本进行了评估。评估使用包含正确结构和相等标准化获取成本的固定硬候选集。平均运行 3 次训练，SI-DQN 将正离子 MS/MS 启动的 Top-1 准确度从 80.10% 提高到 93.78%，将负离子启动的 Top-1 准确度从 70.44% 提高到 93.31%。每个样本相应的平均附加测量计数分别仅为 0.227 和 0.340。完整采集通过四次添加实现了 97.72% 的准确度，而 SI-DQN 将额外测量数量减少了 94.3% 和 91.5%。相对于基于规则的策略，SI-DQN 将终端排名误差和获取成本相结合的综合损失降低了 19.3% 和 25.5%。向 DQN 添加结构信息进一步提高了准确性，同时减少了所有三个训练运行的请求。在可比较的排名不确定性下，SI-DQN 相对于 DQN 在结构性分歧较高的群体中更显着地提高了请求率，同时还观察到复合损失的大幅减少。总之，这些结果支持使用 MS/MS 分析后候选结构之间的模糊性来指导进一步表征，为平衡识别性能与后续测量工作提供定量基础。

English Abstract

Small-molecule identification from tandem mass spectrometry (MS/MS) may require complementary measurements. Herein, we propose a structure-informed deep Q-network (SI-DQN) that uses existing MS/MS evidence to decide whether further characterization is needed, which measurement to request, and when to stop. The policy combines spectral representations and candidate rankings with score-weighted descriptors of the candidates' chemical features and structural differences. The policy was evaluated on 79,462 held-out samples with simulated MS/MS, infrared (IR), and nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) spectra from the Multimodal Spectroscopic Dataset. Evaluation used fixed hard candidate sets containing the correct structure and equal normalized acquisition costs. Averaged across three training runs, SI-DQN increased Top-1 accuracy from 80.10% to 93.78% for positive-ion MS/MS starts and from 70.44% to 93.31% for negative-ion starts. The corresponding mean additional measurement counts were only 0.227 and 0.340 per sample, respectively. Complete acquisition achieved 97.72% accuracy with four additions, whereas SI-DQN reduced the additional measurement count by 94.3% and 91.5%. Relative to a rule-based policy, SI-DQN reduced the composite loss combining terminal ranking error and acquisition cost by 19.3% and 25.5%. Adding structural information to DQN further improved accuracy while reducing requests across all three training runs. At comparable ranking uncertainty, SI-DQN increased request rates relative to DQN more strongly in groups with high structural disagreement, where larger reductions in composite loss were also observed. Together, these results support using the ambiguity among candidate structures after MS/MS analysis to guide further characterization, providing a quantitative basis for balancing identification performance against subsequent measurement effort.

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：材料设计是一个逆问题：找到基态表现出所需特性的微观哈密顿量的耦合。对于强相关的量子系统，这在很大程度上仍然遥不可及，因为每个候选哈密顿量都需要自己昂贵的模拟。在这里，我们展示了基础神经网络量子态（对整个哈密顿量家族的基态进行编码的单波函数）使这种搜索变得高效。由于哈密顿耦合作为神经网络输入，因此任何基态属性都可以直接在耦合空间中通过梯度下降进行优化。我们使用这种方法在维度递增的耦合空间中搜索二维自旋模型中的非磁性相。具体来说，我们专注于…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

材料设计是一个逆问题：找到基态表现出所需特性的微观哈密顿量的耦合。对于强相关的量子系统，这在很大程度上仍然遥不可及，因为每个候选哈密顿量都需要自己昂贵的模拟。在这里，我们展示了基础神经网络量子态（对整个哈密顿量家族的基态进行编码的单波函数）使这种搜索变得高效。由于哈密顿耦合作为神经网络输入，因此任何基态属性都可以直接在耦合空间中通过梯度下降进行优化。我们使用这种方法在维度递增的耦合空间中搜索二维自旋模型中的非磁性相。具体来说，我们专注于量子自旋液体，这是物质的奇异相，其中量子涨落在零温度下阻止磁序。在所考虑的最大搜索空间中，由八个超级交换耦合跨越并包含许多典型的受挫自旋系统，当从磁相开始时，优化收敛到 J1-J2-δ 海森堡模型。我们表明，最佳耦合组的基态在热力学极限内既没有磁序也没有价键序，从而产生了一种新的量子自旋液体候选物。该方法是完全通用的，可以扩展到其他模型和物理属性来优化，证明神经网络量子态不仅是模拟量子物质的工具，也是设计量子物质的工具。

English Abstract

Materials design is an inverse problem: finding the couplings of a microscopic Hamiltonian whose ground state exhibits a desired property. For strongly correlated quantum systems, this remains largely out of reach, as each candidate Hamiltonian requires its own costly simulation. Here we show that Foundation Neural-Network Quantum States, single wave functions that encode the ground states of an entire family of Hamiltonians, make this search efficient. Because the Hamiltonian couplings enter as neural-network inputs, any ground-state property can be optimized by gradient descent directly in coupling space. We use this approach to search for nonmagnetic phases in two-dimensional spin models in coupling spaces of increasing dimensionality. Specifically, we focus on quantum spin liquids, exotic phases of matter in which quantum fluctuations prevent magnetic order at zero temperature. In the largest search space considered, spanned by eight superexchange couplings and containing many paradigmatic frustrated spin systems, the optimization converges to the J₁-J₂-δ Heisenberg model when starting from a magnetic phase. We show that the ground state at the optimum set of couplings has neither magnetic nor valence-bond order in the thermodynamic limit, leading to a new quantum spin-liquid candidate. The approach is completely general and can be extended to other models and physical properties to optimize, demonstrating that Neural-Network Quantum States are tools not only for simulating quantum matter but also for designing it.

无铅 KCuCl3-x 忆阻器中前驱体比例控制的氯空位，用于无成型渐进电阻开关和神经形态学习#

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

忆阻材料的缺陷工程为调节神经形态计算所需的离子传输和模拟电阻开关提供了强大的策略。在这里，我们报告了无铅全无机 KCuCl3-x 薄膜中氯空位（VCl）工程的湿化学合成路线，并建立了缺陷浓度、电子结构、输运动力学和神经形态性能之间的直接相关性。受控的前驱体比率变化可系统地调节 KCuCl3-x 薄膜中的 VCl 浓度，从而驱动末端氯化物位点优先形成空位、带隙变窄并增强电子无序。 W/KCuCl3-x/FTO 忆阻器表现出稳定的双极 VCM 型电阻开关，随着 VCl 浓度的增加，可促进无成型操作。最佳 VCl 浓度（~ 1.3*1021 cm3）可在离子迁移率和缺陷可控性之间实现平衡，从而实现高度线性的增强/抑制特性和卓越的脉冲耐受性（>104 个周期）。当实验测量的优化忆阻器的电导特性被实现为人工神经网络模拟中的突触权重时，在 MNIST 和 Fashion-MNIST 数据集上分别实现了 98.96% 和 91.82% 的识别准确率。这些发现将前体比例控制的 VCl 工程确立为一种有效策略，可同时优化无铅卤化物钙钛矿忆阻器中的离子传输、模拟开关和神经形态学习，为节能神经形态电子器件提供通用材料设计原理。

English Abstract

Defect engineering of memristive materials offers a powerful strategy for regulating ionic transport and analog resistive switching required for neuromorphic computing. Here, we report a wet chemical synthesis route for chloride-vacancy (VCl) engineering in lead-free all-inorganic KCuCl3-x thin films and establish a direct correlation between defect concentration, electronic structure, transport dynamics, and neuromorphic performance. Controlled precursor-ratio variation systematically tunes the VCl concentration in KCuCl3-x thin films, driving preferential vacancy formation at the terminal chloride sites, band-gap narrowing, and enhanced electronic disorder. W/KCuCl3-x/FTO memristors exhibit stable bipolar VCM-type resistive switching, with increasing VCl concentration promoting forming-free operation. An optimal VCl concentration (~ 1.3*10²¹ cm⁻³) provides a balance between ionic mobility and defect controllability, resulting in highly linear potentiation/depression characteristics and superior pulse endurance (>104 cycles). When the experimentally measured conductance characteristics of the optimized memristor are implemented as synaptic weights in artificial neural network simulations, recognition accuracies of 98.96% and 91.82% are achieved on the MNIST and Fashion-MNIST datasets, respectively. These findings establish precursor ratio-controlled VCl engineering as an effective strategy for simultaneously optimizing ionic transport, analog switching, and neuromorphic learning in lead-free halide perovskite memristors, providing a general materials design principle for energy-efficient neuromorphic electronics.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

用新兴材料功能化CMOS的后端（ BEOL ）是神经网络加速器和神经形态系统的关键途径。在这项工作中，我们首次演示了在交付的商用180纳米CMOS模具的BEOL中集成2端铁电突触重量。提出了一种新型材料堆叠，其中WOx/[HfO2-ZrO2]双层夹在TiN和Mo电极之间。对于在450 ℃退火的器件，以低于3 ％的周期间变化实现低于1.8 V的编程。在500 ℃退火的突触重量表现出更多的渐进重量更新，而经过测试的底层NMOS晶体管在完成集成过程后仍保持功能。使用硬件感知神经网络模拟说明了热预算和设备上训练性能之间的权衡。

English Abstract

Functionalising the back-end-of-line (BEOL) of CMOS with emerging materials is a key route to neural network accelerators and neuromorphic systems. In this work, we demonstrate for the first time the integration of 2-terminal ferroelectric synaptic weights in the BEOL of as-delivered commercial 180 nm CMOS dies. A novel material stack is proposed, in which a WOx/[HfO2-ZrO2] bilayer is sandwiched between TiN and Mo electrodes. For devices annealed at 450 degC, programming below 1.8 V is achieved with a cycle-to-cycle variation below 3 %. Synaptic weights annealed at 500 degC exhibit more gradual weight updates, while the tested underlying NMOS transistors remain functional after the complete integration process. The trade-off between thermal budget and on-device training performance is illustrated using hardware-aware neural network simulations.

物理结构作为通用哈密顿学习的组织原则#

原题：Physical structure as an organizing principle for universal Hamiltonian learning
作者：Mingkang Liu、Zhanghao Zhouyin、Guanghui Wang、Daye Zheng、Lei Shen、Qiangqiang Gu。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-28

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory、electronic structure。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

密度泛函理论（DFT）给出了材料的电子结构，但其成本限制了大型超晶胞计算和材料筛选。机器学习的哈密顿量绕过了自洽循环，但通常是特定于系统的；通用的可以涵盖新的组合物和无序固体。此类模型必须能够推广到整个化学领域，并保持下游物理特性，同时保持大电池的廉价性。在这里，我们介绍 UniTB，一个通用的 E(3) 等变哈密顿表示框架，它将植根于密度泛函紧束缚理论的物理先验与深度学习相结合。 UniTB 的网络纠正了先前的哈密顿量，通过由先前的哈密顿量和电荷平衡头路由的专家混合来概括整个化学，就像自洽电荷紧密结合一样。投影限制了先前的能带偏移，抑制了杂散状态并保持了下游特性的物理性。我们为网络提供最快的 SO(2) 张量积 GPU 内核。变体 UniTB-Flash 以 N 原子的 O(N) 成本从两个中心表组装其先验，相对于网格上的 O(Nlog N)。在 29,242 个晶体上进行训练后，UniTB 在跨越 68 个元件的非磁性测试晶体上达到了 0.48 meV 矩阵元件误差。在没有非晶训练数据的情况下，两个模型都符合非晶 Ge-Sb-Te 的 DFT 光学和电导率。 UniTB-Flash对候选者进行排名与UniTB没有显着差异，其属性值更准确；他们共同筛选了 16,195 种晶体，用于五个工业相关应用领域。

English Abstract

Density functional theory (DFT) gives the electronic structure of materials, but its cost limits large-supercell calculations and materials screening. Machine-learned Hamiltonians bypass the self-consistent cycle but are usually system-specific; universal ones could cover new compositions and disordered solids. Such models must generalize across chemistry and keep downstream properties physical, while staying inexpensive for large cells. Here we introduce UniTB, a universal E(3) equivariant Hamiltonian representation framework that integrates physical priors rooted in density-functional tight-binding theory with deep learning. UniTB's network, which corrects a prior Hamiltonian, generalizes across chemistry through a mixture of experts routed by that prior and a charge-equilibration head as in self-consistent-charge tight binding. A projection bounds band shifts from the prior, suppressing spurious states and keeping downstream properties physical. We provide the fastest SO(2) tensor-product GPU kernels for the network. The variant UniTB-Flash assembles its prior from two-center tables at O(N) cost for N atoms, against O(Nlog N) on a grid. Trained on 29,242 crystals, UniTB reaches a 0.48 meV matrix-element error on nonmagnetic test crystals spanning 68 elements. Without amorphous training data, both models agree with DFT optics and conductivity of amorphous Ge-Sb-Te. UniTB-Flash ranks candidates with no significant difference from UniTB, whose property values are more accurate; together they screen 16,195 crystals for five application domains of industrial relevance.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：graph neural network。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们介绍 GMMag，这是一种用于生成晶体结构及其位点解析磁矩的工作流程，构建在带有图神经网络编码器和变压器解码器的条件变分自动编码器上。高斯混合采样将生成重定向到选定的化学和磁性目标，而无需重新训练。我们系统地研究编码器和解码器设计、磁矩幅度和相关符号的表示以及采样选择如何影响重建、生成质量和多样性。由于较高的筛选通过率并不一定会产生更多具有参考数据集中不存在的不同化学式的合格候选者，因此我们模拟了相同化学式的重复生成如何限制其积累，并在两次单独的一千万次抽检运行中测试该模型。作为一种应用，该工作流程可识别参考数据集中不存在的化学式、负形成能和高估计饱和磁化强度的含 Nd 候选物。

English Abstract

We introduce GMMag, a workflow for generating crystal structures together with their site-resolved magnetic moments, built on a conditional variational autoencoder with a graph neural network encoder and a transformer decoder. Gaussian-mixture sampling redirects generation towards selected chemical and magnetic targets without retraining. We systematically investigate how encoder and decoder design, the representation of magnetic-moment magnitudes and relative signs, and sampling choices affect reconstruction, generation quality and diversity. Because a higher screening pass rate does not necessarily yield more qualifying candidates with distinct chemical formulas absent from the reference dataset, we model how repeated generation of the same chemical formulas limits their accumulation and test the model in two separate ten-million-draw runs. As an application, the workflow identifies Nd-containing candidates with chemical formulas absent from the reference dataset, negative formation energies and high estimated saturation magnetization.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

具有大热对比度的可逆固态热开关仍然很少见，但对于先进的热管理至关重要。使用通用机器学习原子间势 (uMLIP) 和密度泛函理论 (DFT)，我们筛选了 57 种过渡金属二硫属化物 (TMDC)，跨越具有 Li 和 Mg 嵌入的 1T 和 2H 相。在这些候选材料中，LiₐZrS2 结合了出色的热导率转换和低热容。锂化成Li0.125ZrS2将横截面热导率从约3.7降低至0.05Wm-1K-1。开关行为超过了之前报道的固态电化学热开关。设备级建模进一步证明了废热回收的潜力，脉冲系统捕获了理想开关回收的 86% 的能量。我们将开关行为归因于 Zr--Zr 框架的显着软化，平均力常数从大约 4.2 eV mathrmmathringA⁻² 降低到 1.2 eV mathrmmathringA⁻²，同时载热声声子的散射增强。这些发现表明电化学插层是层状材料热传输工程的一种有前途的途径。

English Abstract

Reversible solid-state thermal switches with large thermal contrasts remain rare, yet are essential for advanced thermal management. Using universal machine-learned interatomic potentials (uMLIPs) and density functional theory (DFT), we screen 57 transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDCs), spanning the 1T and 2H phases with Li and Mg intercalation. Among the candidates, LiₐZrS₂ combines exceptional thermal conductivity switching with low heat capacity. Lithiation to Li₀.₁₂₅ZrS₂ reduces the cross-plane thermal conductivity from approximately 3.7 to 0.05 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹. The switching behaviour exceeds that of previously reported solid-state electrochemical thermal switches. Device-level modelling further demonstrates the potential for waste-heat recovery, with a pulsed system capturing 86% of the energy recovered by an ideal switch. We attribute the switching behaviour to substantial softening of the Zr--Zr framework, with the mean force constant decreasing from approximately 4.2 to 1.2 eV mathrmmathringA⁻², alongside enhanced scattering of heat-carrying acoustic phonons. These findings establish electrochemical intercalation as a promising route to engineering thermal transport in layered materials.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：electronic structure。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

这项工作描述了基于变分原理的分子反应动力学的几何框架，其中必须求解薛定谔方程才能“了解”反应如何发生。首先，通过讨论最小作用原理和山口定理给出了数学基础。其次，我们讨论物理预备知识，包括推导动能算符（KEO）的等效原理和在一般时空中构建势能面（PES）的人工智能（AI）技术。此外，我们简化了分子系统内弯曲时空的电磁相互作用，因此，我们能够在非零曲率时空中构造核哈密顿量。这表明通过曲率引入规范场的可能性，例如圆锥形交叉点附近的核 KEO 中的附加项。第三，单粒子近似为利用变分原理求解薛定谔方程提供了有力的模拟。因此，我们可以制定电子结构或量子动力学的变分方法。在这项工作中，基于之前的讨论（it Phys. Chem. Chem. Phys. bf 27 (2025), 20397），我们通过几何描述来统一它们，其中自然地引入了几何相位。最后，由于现有理论的优化特点，还从优化的角度对现有理论进行了进一步的讨论，包括两个假设：生成式人工智能技术、扰动的作用以及优化中的马尔可夫过程。

English Abstract

This work describes a geometric framework on molecular reaction dynamics based on the variational principle, where the Schrödinger equation must be solved to ``see'' how a reaction occurs. First, the mathematical preliminaries are given by discussing the principle of least action and the mountain pass theorem. Second, we discuss the physical preliminaries, including the principle of equivalence for deriving the kinetic energy operator (KEO) and artificial intelligence (AI) techniques to build the potential energy surface (PES) in general spacetime. Moreover, we simplified electromagnetic interactions in curved spacetime within the molecular system and consequently, we are able to construct the nuclear Hamiltonian in nonzero curvature spacetime. This indicates possibility to introduce gauge fields through the curvature, such as additional term in the nuclear KEO near a conical intersection. Third, the single-particle approximation provides a powful ansatz to solve the Schrödinger equation by variational principle. Thus, one can formulate the variational approaches for either electronic structure or quantum dynamics. In this work, based on previous discussions (it Phys. Chem. Chem. Phys. bf 27 (2025), 20397) we unified them by a geometric description, where the geometric phase is naturally introduced. Finally, due to optimization characteristic of the present theory, further discussions on the present theory from optimization insight are also given, including two postulates, generative AI techniques, role of perturbation, and Markov process in optimization.

现代太阳能电池模拟中漂移扩散模型的稳定、高阶时间步进方案#

原题：A Stable, High-Order Time-Stepping Scheme for the Drift-Diffusion Model in Modern Solar Cell Simulation
作者：Jun Du、Jun Yan。arXiv · physics，2026-10-27
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：This paper presents a one-dimensional transient drift-diffusion simulator for advanced solar cells, integrating a structure-preserving finite-volume spatial discretization with Scharfetter-Gummel-type fluxes and a high-o…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：perovskite。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

This paper presents a one-dimensional transient drift-diffusion simulator for advanced solar cells, integrating a structure-preserving finite-volume spatial discretization with Scharfetter-Gummel-type fluxes and a high-order, L-stable implicit Runge-Kutta (Radau IIA) temporal integrator. The scheme ensures local charge conservation, handles sharp material interfaces, achieves second-order spatial and fifth-order temporal convergence, and exhibits excellent computational efficiency and numerical stability. Its accuracy is verified against the classical depletion approximation in p-n junction and validated against four independent software implementations for an organic photovoltaic device under a common protocol.该框架的可扩展性通过将激子动力学纳入有机太阳能电池、捕获多时间尺度动力学以及通过对钙钛矿太阳能电池中的移动离子进行建模、在没有经验参数的情况下再现特征J-V磁滞来证明。这项工作为模拟下一代光伏器件中的耦合电荷、激子和离子传输提供了强大的高阶数值基础。

English Abstract

This paper presents a one-dimensional transient drift-diffusion simulator for advanced solar cells, integrating a structure-preserving finite-volume spatial discretization with Scharfetter-Gummel-type fluxes and a high-order, L-stable implicit Runge-Kutta (Radau IIA) temporal integrator. The scheme ensures local charge conservation, handles sharp material interfaces, achieves second-order spatial and fifth-order temporal convergence, and exhibits excellent computational efficiency and numerical stability. Its accuracy is verified against the classical depletion approximation in p-n junction and validated against four independent software implementations for an organic photovoltaic device under a common protocol. The framework's extensibility is demonstrated by incorporating exciton kinetics in organic solar cells, capturing multi-timescale dynamics, and by modeling mobile ions in perovskite solar cells, reproducing characteristic J-V hysteresis without empirical parameters. This work provides a robust, high-order numerical foundation for simulating coupled charge, exciton, and ion transport in next-generation photovoltaic devices.

原子作为代理：集体预测编码的紧急订单#

原题：Atom as an Agent: Emergent Order by Collective Predictive Coding
作者：Koji Hashimoto、Tadahiro Taniguchi。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-28

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

微观系统中出现宏观序参数的内在原因是什么？在本文中，我们将集体预测编码（CPC），即语言/符号出现理论应用于物理学。正如我们的语言源于人类的交流，物理宏观秩序参数也源于原子之间的交流——每个原子都被视为一个通过推理来预测彼此行为的代理。我们提出了经典自旋系统的三个例子：二维铁磁和反铁磁 Ising 模型、具有竞争条纹顺序的二维自旋模型以及完全连接的 Sherrington-Kirkpatrick 自旋玻璃模型。在所有这些例子中，包括副本重叠在内的适当的宏观顺序参数仅通过观察到的自旋配置通过 CPC 的通信信息自动出现。我们将学习到的宏观坐标称为集体预测顺序。此外，对于铁磁和反铁磁伊辛模型，集体预测顺序的平衡分布产生了具有朗道势的对称性和热演化特征的涌现势。值得注意的是，集体预测秩序的出现并不涉及微观哈密顿量/拉格朗日量的任何结构。因此，我们的“原子作为代理”（AaaA）原理为宏观秩序的出现提供了一种完全数据驱动的方法，即机器学习物理学。

English Abstract

What is the intrinsic reason of emergence of macroscopic order parameter out of microscopic systems? In this article, we apply to physics the collective predictive coding (CPC), the theory of language/symbol emergence. As our language emerges out of our human communications, physical macroscopic order parameter emerges out of communications among atoms --- each atom is regarded as an agent who predicts each other's behavior through inference. We present three examples of classical spin systems: two-dimensional ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic Ising models, a two-dimensional spin model with competing stripe orders, and the fully connected Sherrington--Kirkpatrick spin-glass model. In all of these examples, proper macroscopic order parameters including the replica overlap emerge automatically through communication informatics of CPC, only from observed spin configurations. We call the learned macroscopic coordinate the collective predictive order. Furthermore, for the ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic Ising models, the equilibrium distribution of the collective predictive order yields an emergent potential with the symmetry and thermal evolution characteristic of a Landau potential. Notably, the emergence of the collective predictive order does not refer to any structure of microscopic Hamiltonians/Lagrangians. Thus our ``Atom-as-an-Agent" (AaaA) principle offers a completely data-driven method for the emergence of macroscopic order, that is, machine learning physics.

分子聚集体拓扑边缘态的近场图#

原题：Near-field maps of topological edge states of molecular aggregates
作者：Aritra Mishra、Sidhartha Nayak、Alexander Eisfeld。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-26

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

自组装二维分子聚集体为工程激子态（包括拓扑非平凡边缘态）提供了一个通用平台。然而，这些边缘态很难用光学方法探测：它们的远场跃迁强度较弱，并且远场光谱无法揭示激发是否局部在边缘。对于由两个具有真正复杂的跃迁偶极子的分子亚晶格组成的聚集体，我们表明空间分辨近场光谱编码了这些状态的完整空间结构。由此产生的近场地图并不反映现场人口，而是表现出强烈的不均匀、类似干扰的特征。我们证明，卷积神经网络可以从这些图中重建本征态的复展开系数，并且这种重建对于大量随机噪声具有鲁棒性。

English Abstract

Self-assembled two-dimensional molecular aggregates provide a versatile platform for engineering excitonic states, including topologically nontrivial edge states. However, these edge-states are difficult to probe optically: they have weak far-field transition strengths, and far-field spectra do not reveal whether an excitation is localized at the edge. For an aggregate consisting of two sublattices of molecules with genuinely complex transition dipoles, we show that spatially resolved near-field spectroscopy encodes the full spatial structure of these states. The resulting near-field maps do not mirror the site populations, but instead exhibit strongly nonuniform, interference-like features. We demonstrate that a convolutional neural network can reconstruct the complex expansion coefficients of the eigenstates from these maps, and that this reconstruction is robust against substantial random noise.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

天王星和海王星异常磁场的起源，具有显着的倾斜和多偶极结构，对于理解冰巨星的内部结构和演化至关重要。最近的研究证实，超离子 H2O 冰具有热力学稳定性，并构成其冰幔中的主要 H2O 相。在这里，我们使用深度学习和从头算分子动力学来研究超离子 H2O 冰的热和质子输运特性。我们证明超离子 H2O 冰表现出明显的质子热电效应，在天王星内部的最大塞贝克系数可以达到 sim 620 μV K⁻1，而海王星的塞贝克系数较低，在 570-585 μV K⁻1 范围内。因此，冰幔中的温度梯度可以引起质子迁移，从而驱动磁场的产生。基于这一机制，在捕获平均场的偶极近似中，天王星和海王星之间平均偶极磁场强度的差异可以完全用它们不同的内部温度梯度来解释，并且预测的平均场强度与航海家二号观测到的平均场强度相当。

English Abstract

The origin of the anomalous magnetic fields of Uranus and Neptune, with significant tilts and multi-dipole configurations, is central to understanding the internal structure and evolution of the ice giants. Recent investigations confirmed that superionic H₂O ice is thermodynamically stable and constitutes the dominant H₂O phase within their icy mantles. Here, we use deep learning and ab initio molecular dynamics to investigate the thermal and protonic transport properties of superionic H₂O ice. We demonstrate that superionic H₂O ice exhibits a pronounced protonic thermoelectric effect, with a maximum Seebeck coefficient that can reach sim 620 μV K⁻¹ within the interior of Uranus, whereas Neptune's is lower, within the range of 570-585 μV K⁻¹. Consequently, the temperature gradients in the icy mantles can induce proton migration, thereby driving magnetic field generation. Based on this mechanism, and within a dipolar approximation that captures the mean field, the disparities in mean dipolar magnetic field strength between Uranus and Neptune can be explained exclusively by their differing internal temperature gradients, and the predicted mean field strengths are comparable to those observed by Voyager 2.

掩埋界面分子态的亚单层跟踪#

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

新兴技术的现象源于埋藏界面处的新颖分子状态。然而，在大多数情况下，定量解决这些特征是不可能的。共振散射已证明对这些局部状态敏感，但由于缺乏准确的解释模型而受到限制。我们利用机器学习 (ML) 优化的光学模型来分析分子薄膜的偏振共振软 X 射线散射 (RSoXS)。这项新分析建立了分子取向、化学特性和堆积状态作为深度函数的直接定量解释，具有亚单层分辨率——通过光学模型锁定能量和偏振，超出了衍射极限。从我们的分析中，我们发现界面上出现的分子取向和堆积的梯度演化，表明表面与整体生长动力学的竞争以及基材图案化的相互矛盾的假设。这种对埋藏界面形成和最终状态的非破坏性洞察为控制下一代技术中的界面状态和现象提供了可能性。

English Abstract

Phenomena responsible for emerging technologies originate from novel molecular states at buried interfaces. Yet quantitatively resolving these features is out of reach in most cases. Resonant scattering has demonstrated sensitivity to these local states but is limited due to a lack of accurate interpretive models. We leverage a machine learning (ML)-optimized optical model to analyze polarized resonant soft X-ray scattering (RSoXS) from molecular thin films. This new analysis establishes the direct and quantitative interpretation of molecular orientation, chemical identity and packing states as a function of depth with sub-monolayer resolution - reaching beyond the diffraction limit via locking energy and polarization through the optical model. From our analysis, we discover a gradient evolution of molecular orientation and packing emerging from the interface, indicative of competing surface versus bulk growth dynamics and contradicting hypotheses of substrate patterning. This nondestructive insight into buried interfacial formation and final state opens possibilities in controlling interfacial states and phenomena in next-generation technologies.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

超级布洛赫振荡（SBO）提供了控制驱动晶格中波包传播的强大途径，但它们在磁波系统中的实现仍然难以捉摸。在这里，我们预测了由磁各向异性场的静态和交流梯度驱动的周期性磁振子晶体中磁振子波包的 SBO。仅静态梯度就会引起具有恒定载波频率的磁振子布洛赫振荡 (BO)。值得注意的是，添加近谐振 MHz 级交流驱动会产生缓慢的大幅度包络振荡，这是 SBO 的标志。在精确谐振时，交流驱动可实现相控定向传输，载波频率偏移高达 GHz 级。因此，相同的结构可以在三种不同的机制之间切换：常规 BO、SBO 和定向运输。通过微磁模拟验证了振荡幅度、超周期、漂移速度和频率演化的解析表达式。扩展到反铁磁体磁振子晶体，我们进一步证明这些效应在太赫兹范围内驱动两个相反极化的磁振子模式的相反运动。我们的工作将 magnon SBO 打造为一个多功能平台，为可重新配置的 magnon 设备开辟了一条新途径。

English Abstract

Super-Bloch oscillations (SBOs) offer a powerful route to control wave packet propagation in driven lattices, yet their realization in magnonic systems has remained elusive. Here, we predict SBOs of magnon wave packets in periodic magnonic crystals driven by static and ac gradients of magnetic anisotropy field. A static gradient alone induces magnon Bloch oscillation (BO) with a constant carrier frequency. Remarkably, adding a near-resonant MHz-scale ac driving gives rise to a slow large-amplitude envelope oscillation, the hallmark of SBO. At exact resonance, the ac driving enables phase-controlled directional transport with a carrier-frequency shift up to GHz scale. Consequently, the same structure can be switched among three distinct regimes: conventional BO, SBO, and directed transport. Analytical expressions for the oscillation amplitudes, superperiod, drift velocity, and frequency evolution are validated by micromagnetic simulations. Extending to antiferromagnet magnonic crystals, we further demonstrate that these effects drive opposite motions of two oppositely polarized magnon modes in THz range. Our work establishes magnon SBOs as a versatile platform, opening a new avenue for reconfigurable magnonic devices.

QuantumMatterExplorer：用于发现物质的量子多体相的自主代理#

原题：QuantumMatterExplorer: An Autonomous Agent for the Discovery of Quantum Many-Body Phases of Matter
作者：Jannes Nys、Andrew Jreissaty、Juan Carrasquilla。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-26

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：绘制新量子哈密顿量的相图需要在每一步都进行专家判断：测量哪些可观测值，在参数空间中的何处进行计算，或者如何将真实的顺序与有限尺寸的人工制品分开。我们引入了 QuantumMatterExplorer，这是一个自主代理，它仅将哈密顿量及其参数范围作为输入，并返回从其自己的数值模拟中获得的相图。该代理建立在大型语言模型的基础上，旨在进行长期推理、维护持久的书面计划并驱动一组计算工具，并通过密度矩阵重整化群 (DMRG) 计算与独立精确对角化…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

绘制新量子哈密顿量的相图需要在每一步都进行专家判断：测量哪些可观测值，在参数空间中的何处进行计算，或者如何将真实的顺序与有限尺寸的人工制品分开。我们引入了 QuantumMatterExplorer，这是一个自主代理，它仅将哈密顿量及其参数范围作为输入，并返回从其自己的数值模拟中获得的相图。该代理建立在大型语言模型的基础上，旨在进行长期推理、维护持久的书面计划并驱动一组计算工具，并通过密度矩阵重整化群 (DMRG) 计算与独立精确对角化进行交叉检查。该代理将大型语言模型与持久规划、专家计算物理技能库以及针对独立精确对角化进行验证的密度矩阵重整化群计算相结合。它自适应地选择要评估的数量、细化参数扫描，并随着证据的积累执行有限大小的分析。在用已知跃迁验证哈密顿量的方法后，我们用具有单离子各向异性的受挫双线性双二次自旋 1 链来挑战它，其二维相图最近才发布，不太可能出现在任何训练语料库中。在没有任何关于预期物理现象的提示的情况下，代理会提出要探测哪些可观测值，并识别候选 Néel、Haldane 和二聚相，与参考相图大致一致。它还可以检测不相称的相关性并推断相边界的受挫相关几何形状，所有这些都在八小时的计算预算内。这些结果为量子物质新相的自主计算发现开辟了一条实用途径。

English Abstract

Mapping the phase diagram of a new quantum Hamiltonian demands expert judgement at every step: which observables to measure, where in parameter space to compute, or how to separate genuine order from finite-size artefacts. We introduce QuantumMatterExplorer, an autonomous agent that takes only a Hamiltonian and its parameter ranges as input and returns a phase diagram obtained from its own numerical simulations. The agent is built on a large language model and is designed to reason over a long horizon, maintain a persistent written plan, and drive a set of computational tools, with density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) calculations cross-checked against independent exact diagonalization. The agent combines a large language model with persistent planning, a library of expert computational-physics skills, and density-matrix renormalization group calculations validated against independent exact diagonalization. It adaptively selects quantities to evaluate, refines parameter scans, and performs finite-size analyses as evidence accumulates. After validating the approach on Hamiltonians with known transitions, we challenge it with a frustrated bilinear-biquadratic spin-1 chain with single-ion anisotropy, whose two-dimensional phase diagram was published only recently and is unlikely to appear in any training corpus. Given no hint about the expected physics, the agent proposes which observables to probe, and identifies candidate Néel, Haldane and dimerized phases, in broad agreement with the reference phase diagram. It also detects incommensurate correlations and infers the frustration-dependent geometry of the phase boundaries, all within an eight-hour compute budget. These results open a practical route to the autonomous computational discovery of new phases of quantum matter.

磁惯性聚变等离子体中快速α粒子传输的扩散模型#

原题：Diffusion model for fast alpha-particle transport in Magneto-Inertial Fusion plasmas

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

开发了一种扩散模型来描述密集磁化 DT 聚变等离子体中的快速 α 粒子传输和限制，该模型专为磁惯性聚变条件而定制。该模型使用 Chapman-Enskog 近似从 Fokker-Planck 动力学方程导出，包括电子和离子的动态库仑摩擦。这种方法改进了早期的启发式模型 [Liberman 和 Velikovich，J. Plasma Phys。 31, 369 (1984)]，虽然在接近 5 keV 的点火范围内有效，但在高增益磁化线性惯性聚变 (MagLIF) 所需的约 20 keV 较高温度下缺乏精度。本文提供了所有输运系数的明确解析公式，并描述了磁化等离子体中 α 粒子压力梯度和相关安培力之间的关系，这两者都与高温下的 MagLIF 相关。

English Abstract

A diffusion model is developed to describe fast alpha-particle transport and confinement in dense magnetized DT fusion plasmas, tailored to Magneto-Inertial Fusion conditions. The model, derived from the Fokker-Planck kinetic equation using the Chapman-Enskog approximation, includes dynamic Coulomb friction from electrons and ions. This approach improves upon earlier heuristic model [Liberman and Velikovich, J. Plasma Phys. 31, 369 (1984)], which, while valid in the ignition range near 5 keV, lacks accuracy at the higher temperatures ~20 keV required for high-gain Magnetized Liner Inertial Fusion (MagLIF). This paper provides explicit analytic formulas for all transport coefficients and characterizes the relationship between the alpha-particle pressure gradient and the associated Ampère force in magnetized plasmas, which are both relevant for MagLIF at high temperatures.

中文摘要

传统上，设计电力电子转换器需要在拓扑尺寸、商业元件选择、电路仿真验证和原理图起草方面具有丰富的人类专业知识。虽然大型语言模型 (LLM) 在电子设计自动化 (EDA) 领域展现出良好的前景，但将独立的 LLM 直接应用于板级电力电子原理图综合会遭受严重的工程幻觉，包括分析尺寸不准确、制造或不匹配的元件零件号、违反电压降额裕度以及破坏的拓扑连接。在本文中，我们提出了一个有界双环自动原理图合成框架，该框架将自然语言规范转换为经过验证的、可编辑的 KiCad 原理图和制造可交付成果。我们的工作流程集成了三个功能列的六个阶段：（1）需求解析和知识驱动的功率级综合，涵盖非隔离（降压、升压）和隔离/谐振（反激、DAB、半桥 LLC）拓扑； (2) 基于分销商 API 和 OEM 数据表的合同和证据协调的组件选择，加上闭环 ngspice 瞬态验证和压力反馈； (3) 模块化控制硬件集成和多格式可交付成果生成，具有本地 KiCad 网表往返验证功能。对 24-36V 至 12V、2A 降压转换器（29 个位置，83 个验证端子）和隔离式 48V 至 24V、0.5A、双有源桥 (DAB) 转换器（87 个位置，341 个验证端子）的端到端案例研究，以及与 Vanilla LLM 基线的比较评估，表明我们的框架将组件验证覆盖范围从 22.2 提高到 98.6，标称值一致性从 0到 97.7，SPICE 仿真通过率从 0 到 100。

English Abstract

Designing power electronics converters traditionally requires intensive human expertise across topology sizing, commercial component selection, circuit simulation verification, and schematic drafting. While Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise in electronic design automation (EDA), applying standalone LLMs directly to board-level power electronics schematic synthesis suffers from severe engineering hallucinations, including inaccurate analytical sizing, fabricated or mismatched component part numbers, violated voltage derating margins, and broken topological connectivity. In this paper, we propose a Bounded Dual-Loop Automated Schematic Synthesis Framework that translates natural language specifications into verified, editable KiCad schematics and manufacturing deliverables. Our workflow integrates six stages across three functional columns: (1) requirement parsing and knowledge-driven power stage synthesis covering non-isolated (Buck, Boost) and isolated/resonant (Flyback, DAB, half-bridge LLC) topologies; (2) contract- and evidence-coordinated component selection grounded in distributor APIs and OEM datasheets, coupled with closed-loop ngspice transient verification and stress feedback; and (3) modular control hardware integration and multi-format deliverable generation featuring native KiCad netlist round-trip verification. End-to-end case studies on a 24-36V to 12V,2A Buck converter (29 positions, 83 verified terminals) and an isolated 48V to 24V, 0.5A, Dual-Active Bridge (DAB) converter (87 positions, 341 verified terminals), alongside comparative evaluations against a Vanilla LLM baseline, demonstrate that our framework improves component verification coverage from 22.2 to 98.6, nominal value consistency from 0 to 97.7, and SPICE simulation pass rates from 0 to 100.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

钙钛矿已被研究用于广泛的应用，从氧化物基铁电体到卤化物钙钛矿光伏发电。尽管它们具有多种功能，但这些材料由于结构不稳定性（例如极性偏心和八面体倾斜）而具有很强的晶格非和谐性。这些非谐波失真强烈影响低频振动响应，可以在红外 (IR) 和拉曼光谱中观察到。然而，低频红外和拉曼特征通常是分开研究的，这阻碍了对钙钛矿类振动光谱的统一理解的发展。在这项工作中，我们使用基于机器学习的分子动力学来模拟氧化物钙钛矿（SrTiO₃和BaTiO₃）、卤化物钙钛矿（CsPbBr₃、CsSnBr₃和CsGeBr₃）和双卤化物钙钛矿（Cs2AgAlBr₆和Cs2AgBiBr₆）的红外和拉曼光谱。通过比较表现出极性、倾斜驱动和混合不稳定性的材料，我们发现极性偏心会导致低频红外和介电响应增强，而非极性模式，特别是八面体倾斜，与拉曼中心峰相关。低频特征的强度还取决于底层模式与极化或介电磁化率耦合的强度。最后，我们表明这些不稳定性的动力学起着重要作用，缓慢的极性重新定向将介电响应转移到非常低的频率，并且过阻尼倾斜模式产生持续的拉曼中心峰。这些结果建立了一个统一的框架，将低频红外和拉曼光谱与其潜在的非谐晶格不稳定性的对称性、耦合和动力学联系起来。

English Abstract

Perovskites have been studied for a wide range of applications, from oxide-based ferroelectrics to halide-perovskite photovoltaics. Despite their diverse functionalities, these materials share strong lattice anharmonicity arising from structural instabilities such as polar off-centering and octahedral tilting. These anharmonic distortions strongly influence the low-frequency vibrational response and can be observed in infrared (IR) and Raman spectra. However, low-frequency IR and Raman signatures are typically investigated separately, hindering the development of a unified understanding of vibrational spectroscopy across perovskite classes. In this work, we use machine-learning-based molecular dynamics to simulate the IR and Raman spectra of oxide perovskites (SrTiO₃ and BaTiO₃), halide perovskites (CsPbBr₃, CsSnBr₃, and CsGeBr₃) and double halide perovskites (Cs₂AgAlBr₆ and Cs₂AgBiBr₆). By comparing materials exhibiting polar, tilt-driven and mixed instabilities, we find that polar off-centering leads to enhanced low-frequency IR and dielectric responses, whereas nonpolar modes, particularly octahedral tilting, are associated with Raman central peaks. The strength of the low-frequency features also depends on how strongly the underlying modes couple to polarization or dielectric susceptibility. Finally, we show that the dynamics of these instabilities play an important role, with slow polar reorientations shifting the dielectric response to very low frequencies and overdamped tilting modes producing persistent Raman central peaks. These results establish a unified framework connecting low-frequency IR and Raman spectra to the symmetry, coupling and dynamics of their underlying anharmonic lattice instabilities.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

脉冲溅射工艺对于从硬涂层到微电子的广泛应用至关重要。高功率脉冲磁控溅射 (HiPIMS) 由于其高电离通量分数，为工艺设计提供了独特的可能性。由于涉及高峰值电流密度，相应地高等离子体密度会产生许多离子。这使得能够通过施加加速偏置电势来调节动能。然而，一些 HiPIMS 工艺的沉积速率较低，因为这些离子与带负电的溅射靶材受到反向吸引力。 HiPIMS 最近的工作针对这个问题，探索复杂的脉冲方案，例如双极、多脉冲或斩波 HiPIMS。这些脉冲方案引入了另一个问题：工艺复杂性。因此，即使间隔较粗，进程空间也很快会增加到 1E5-1E20 个可能的进程组合。这就需要高效、智能的采样，这可以通过贝叶斯优化等机器学习算法来实现。在这项工作中，我们使用多目标贝叶斯优化（MOBO）来有效地对脉冲包的过程空间进行采样，以高电离通量分数和沉积速率为目标。在这次探索过程中，我们对 2,000 多个不同的过程进行了采样。令人惊讶的是，我们发现许多 HiPIMS 工艺（包括单脉冲）可以实现高于 DC 参考的沉积速率。事实上，在 1.0 Pa 的工作压力和 5-20% 的中等电离率下，我们发现毫秒级脉冲是理想的。我们假设这些过程最大化了气体稀疏，在目标和基底之间创建了低碰撞通道。这些结果展示了以前未报道的脉冲溅射工艺的行为，并挑战了薄膜制造中薄膜质量和速度之间长期存在的权衡。

English Abstract

Pulsed sputter processes are essential for a wide range of applications from hard coatings to microelectronics. High power impulse magnetron sputtering (HiPIMS) offers unique possibilities for process design due to its high ionized flux fraction. Because of the high peak-current densities involved, correspondingly high plasma densities generate many ions. This enables tuning of kinetic energies by applying accelerating bias potentials. However, some HiPIMS processes have low deposition rates due to the back-attraction these ions experience with the negatively charged sputter target. Recent work in HiPIMS has targeted this problem, exploring complex pulsing schemes such as bipolar, multi-pulse, or chopped HiPIMS. These pulsing schemes introduce another problem: process complexity. As such, the process space, even with coarse intervals quickly blows up to 1E5-1E20 possible process combinations. This calls for efficient and intelligent sampling, which can be enabled by machine-learning algorithms such as Bayesian optimization. In this work we use multi-objective Bayesian optimization (MOBO) to efficiently sample the process space of pulse-packets, targeting high ionization flux fractions and deposition rates. Over 2,000 distinct processes were sampled during this exploration. Surprisingly, we found that many HiPIMS processes, including single pulse, can achieve deposition rates higher than DC references. In fact, at a working pressure of 1.0 Pa and moderate ionization rates of 5-20% we found that ms-scale pulses were ideal. We hypothesize these processes maximize gas rarefaction, creating a low-collision channel between the target and the substrate. These results showcase previously unreported behavior of pulsed sputter processes and challenge a long-held trade-off between film quality and speed in thin-film manufacturing.

ACAM-J-MARS: Multi-Atlas Riemannian Spectral Representations for Decoding Advanced Meditation States from fMRI#

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Inferring jhana-type advanced concentration absorption meditation (ACAM-J) states from fMRI data is challenging due to the spatially distributed nature of meditation-related effects, substantial inter-individual variability that can exceed within-subject state differences, and limited sample sizes. Conventional machine-learning approaches often formulate this problem as unimodal, Euclidean, atlas-based classification, thereby neglecting the non-Euclidean geometry of functional connectivity matrices and underrepresenting temporal information in functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI); these limitations can lead to suboptimal classification performance. We propose ACAM-J-MARS (Multi-Atlas Riemannian Spectral representation), which computes functional connectivity using multiple atlases, represents symmetric positive definite (SPD) connectivity matrices on a Riemannian manifold, projects them into a log-Euclidean tangent space, and augments connectivity-derived features with low-frequency BOLD spectral energy to better resolve subtle state differences under heterogeneous variability. Evaluated on 204 segments from 22 practitioners, ACAM-J-MARS improves performance across multiple backbone models, achieving a balanced accuracy of 0.757 for early versus late ACAM-J classification and 0.545 for three-class classification, corresponding to a 10.8-point absolute improvement over the strongest baseline. These findings suggest that the proposed representation improves the decoding of advanced meditative brain states and may enable downstream applications such as closed-loop neurofeedback and real-time psychophysiological brain-computer interfaces.

Benchmarking Graph Neural Networks for AC Power Flow: The Role of Inductive Biases and Physical Constraints#

作者：Federico Clerici、Ferran Bohigas-Daranas、Hamid Latif-Martínez、Eduardo Prieto-Araujo、Oriol Gomis-Bellmunt、Pere Barlet-Ros。arXiv · cs.LG，2026-10-30

📄 摘要：Fast and accurate models for AC power flow (ACPF) are increasingly important as modern electrical grids require more frequent system analyses. Graph neural networks (GNNs) naturally exploit electrical grid topology, yet…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：graph neural networks。

阅读原文 ↗

English Abstract

Fast and accurate models for AC power flow (ACPF) are increasingly important as modern electrical grids require more frequent system analyses. Graph neural networks (GNNs) naturally exploit electrical grid topology, yet the effects of architectural design choices on predictive performance remain poorly understood given differences in graph representations and evaluation settings across studies. We present a unified benchmark of representative GNN architectures for ACPF prediction under a common experimental framework. All models use identical preprocessing and training procedures and comparable parameter budgets, isolating the impact of message-passing mechanisms and inductive biases. We also analyze errors by distance from the slack bus and systematically ablate key design components. We evaluate predictive accuracy and physical consistency on the IEEE 14-bus and IEEE 118-bus systems. Results indicate that anisotropic message-passing architectures offer the most favorable trade-off between predictive accuracy and physical consistency under the studied parameter and topology constraints. Explicit edge features encoding line impedances and global context aggregation are particularly important for representing electrical interactions and system-wide dependencies, especially for voltage-angle predictions on the topologically bottlenecked IEEE-118 grid. Under a budget with approximately fixed parameters, two or three message-passing layers generally provide the best balance between depth and representation width, while additional depth offers no consistent improvement. Ablations also show that standard residual and root connections are not necessarily beneficial in shallow models, and that aggregation operators involve trade-offs between statistical accuracy and physical consistency. These results provide practical guidelines for designing graph-based power system models.

A fully convolutional neural network to classify UXOs using multicomponent electromagnetic induction data#

作者：Jorge Lopez-Alvis、Lindsey J. Heagy、Douglas W. Oldenburg、Stephen D. Billings、Lin-Ping Song、Wouter Deleersnyder。arXiv · physics，2026-10-30
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配6

📄 摘要：Unexploded ordnance (UXO) clearance relies on electromagnetic induction (EMI) methods to detect and classify buried and submerged metallic objects, but the standard classification workflow requires physics-based inversio…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Unexploded ordnance (UXO) clearance relies on electromagnetic induction (EMI) methods to detect and classify buried and submerged metallic objects, but the standard classification workflow requires physics-based inversion and manual expert review. We present a fully convolutional neural network (CNN) that classifies UXO directly from multicomponent time-domain EMI data, bypassing the inversion step entirely. The CNN uses a segmentation-like architecture that preserves the spatial dimensions of the input and outputs a high-resolution classification map suitable for generating a diglist. Two CNNs are used in sequence: the first detects anomalies due to metallic objects and characterizes the spatially correlated noise present in the preprocessed field data; the second performs multi-class classification using synthetic training data augmented with that noise. A physics-based parameterization of the clutter class is used to train the network to discriminate clutter from UXO across a broad range of object types. We validate this approach on two field datasets acquired with the UltraTEMA-4 system at the Sequim Bay marine test site. On a calibration grid with 21 seeded objects, the CNN detects all UXOs and discriminates two out of four clutter objects. On an independent blind grid with 35 seeded objects (17 UXOs, 18 clutter), the CNN detects and classifies all UXOs and achieves a false-positive count comparable to that of the physics-based inversion approach. We see the two methods as complementary: once trained, the CNN classifies rapidly and with minimal supervision, making it well suited as a fast first-pass tool or as an independent check on physics-based results.

Scaling and Renormalization in Neural Networks: An Effective Renormalization-Group Description of Grokking#

作者：Min Chen、Junqi Wang、Zeguan Wu、Wenhao Huang、Junyu Liu。arXiv · cs.LG，2026-10-28
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配6

📄 摘要：Grokking is a delayed transition from memorization to generalization in deep neural networks (NNs). We examine whether scaling and renormalization group (RG) theories can describe this dynamics, establishing a quantitati…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Grokking is a delayed transition from memorization to generalization in deep neural networks (NNs). We examine whether scaling and renormalization group (RG) theories can describe this dynamics, establishing a quantitative physical framework for neural generalization transitions. For modular addition on a two-layer NN, an ensemble of trained models defines an order parameter (the squared weight norm) and fluctuation proxies for the heat capacity and the susceptibility, with training epoch and weight decay serving as thermal and field control coordinates. To bridge neural architectures to real-space RG, we construct an exact coupling matrix via a second-order expansion of the loss with respect to per-neuron norms and use its Fiedler vector to define an effective geometry. This spectral ordering yields a monotonic spatial decay of coupling strength that is absent under random permutations. We then execute real-space RG by block-spin aggregating adjacent neurons and parameterizing the coupling matrix into off-diagonal band strengths. Linearizing this local RG map yields relevant scaling exponents and five derived critical exponents, with direct empirical estimates matching RG predictions within 1-10%. Overall, our framework provides a consistent effective description of grokking, suggesting the scaling and renormalization group formalism as a tool to investigate NN dynamics.

MINT: Mitigating Structural Inconsistency via Attentive Message Passing for Financial Anomaly Detection#

作者：Yin-Ju Wu、Bi-Cheng Yan、Berlin Chen。arXiv · cs.LG，2026-10-27
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配6

📄 摘要：Illicit account detection is a critical task for financial risk control systems, aiming to pinpoint malicious actors within transaction networks. Existing efforts typically recast transaction networks into directed multi…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：graph neural networks。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Illicit account detection is a critical task for financial risk control systems, aiming to pinpoint malicious actors within transaction networks. Existing efforts typically recast transaction networks into directed multigraphs and leverage graph neural networks (GNNs) to detect anomalies via topological structures under the homophily assumption. However, directly grafting GNNs onto transaction graphs is faced with at least two pivotal challenges, i.e., structural inconsistency and label scarcity. The former arises from deceptive connections forged by malicious actors, while the latter stems from the lack of annotated data. In light of these, we introduce MINT, a novel graph neural framework that mitigates structural inconsistency via an attentive message-passing scheme on transaction graphs. Specifically, MINT introduces a discrepancy-aware feature propagation, which tracks malicious actors by quantifying deviations between nodes across bi-directional transaction flows. A transaction salience module is proposed to mitigate structural inconsistency by adaptively regularizing the interaction strength between nodes. Furthermore, to alleviate label scarcity, MINT is optimized via Positive-Unlabeled (PU) learning, which exploits only a minority of illicit nodes coupled with a majority of unlabeled ones. Extensive experiments conducted on three financial transaction benchmarks, including two large-scale public and one in-house real-world datasets, validate the superior effectiveness of MINT over several cutting-edge baselines.

中文摘要

分子晶体中的多态性对控制结晶提出了重大挑战。在这里，我们结合实验和理论来表征吖啶的多晶型物景观，并研究其添加剂的选择性控制。熔体结晶实验解决了形式VIII的先前未知结构，而拓扑晶体结构预测恢复了具有确定晶体结构的所有七种已知未溶解多晶型物。将微调的机器学习原子间势能与非谐波自由能计算相结合，再现了实验观察到的稳定性关系，并揭示了浅层自由能景观。最后，实验观察到的添加剂诱导的多晶型物偏好与混合晶体替代缺陷能量以及添加剂和吖啶晶体结构之间的拓扑相似性相关。总之，这些结果提供了吖啶多态性、连接晶体结构、热力学稳定性和添加剂控制结晶的全面图景。

English Abstract

Polymorphism in molecular crystals presents a major challenge for controlling crystallization. Here, we combine experiment and theory to characterize the polymorph landscape of acridine and investigate its selective control by additives. Melt crystallization experiments resolve the previously unknown structure of form VIII, while topological crystal structure prediction recovers all seven known unsolvated polymorphs with determined crystal structures. Combining a fine-tuned machine-learned interatomic potential with anharmonic free energy calculations reproduces the experimentally observed stability relationships and reveals a shallow free energy landscape. Finally, experimentally observed additive-induced polymorph preferences correlate with mixed-crystal substitutional defect energies and topological similarities between additive and acridine crystal structures. Together, these results provide a comprehensive picture of acridine polymorphism, linking crystal structure, thermodynamic stability, and additive-controlled crystallization.

Generative modeling for fusion: a large-scale benchmark of stellarator fast-ion simulations#

作者：Misha Padidar、Teresa Huang、Mark Goldstein、Michael Czekanski、Jeffrey Soules。arXiv · physics，2026-10-30
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配3

📄 摘要：Stellarators offer a promising magnetic confinement approach for achieving controlled nuclear fusion. However, stellarator design remains challenging, as evaluating configuration performance requires computationally expe…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Stellarators offer a promising magnetic confinement approach for achieving controlled nuclear fusion. However, stellarator design remains challenging, as evaluating configuration performance requires computationally expensive, high-fidelity simulations. In particular, key performance metrics derived from guiding center simulations are costly to include directly in the design optimization loop, and are almost exclusively used as diagnostics. Machine learning has the potential to advance fusion research by enabling rapid generation and evaluation of new stellarator configurations. Effectively applying ML for fusion demands large-scale datasets that capture both design diversity and high-fidelity simulation outputs. In this work, we introduce the Benchmark of Stellarator Simulations (BoSS), a dataset of stellarator designs paired with guiding center simulation results and trajectories. The BoSS is the first large-scale dataset of guiding center simulations, containing simulation data for 180,000 distinct magnetohydrodynamic equilibria spanning a wide range of magnetic geometries and confinement properties. To exemplify the utility of ML to accelerate stellarator design, we formulate and address two sets of modeling tasks: generative modeling for designing stellarators with good particle confinement, and surrogate modeling for predicting stellarator heat deposition patterns and particle trajectories. The tasks reveal the potential impact ML can have in the stellarator field and important opportunities to improve the applicability of ML models in the sciences. Our baselines generate diverse, performant stellarators and provide useful surrogate predictions, and point to long-horizon trajectory forecasting and out-of-distribution generation as key directions for future work. The BoSS can be accessed through https://boss.flatironinstitute.org/.

中文摘要

本章探讨了新兴的非易失性存储器（eNVM）设备在推进神经形态计算方面的关键作用。受人脑执行模式识别和适应等复杂任务的非凡能力的启发，神经形态计算为在实际硬件中实现复杂的神经网络 (NN) 提供了巨大的潜力。尽管对大脑复杂性的研究正在进行中，但其基本运作依赖于神经元（通过电化学信号进行通信的处理单元）和突触（神经元之间的连接）。突触的可改变强度是学习和记忆功能的关键，使大脑能够适应和重新布线。对于神经形态计算中的密集和低功耗电路，这些 eNVM 设备由于其类似突触的行为和非易失性信息存储而成为理想选择。这些设备的电导调制等特性可以模拟突触可塑性，这对于学习至关重要，而且它们的可扩展性使它们有望用于大规模神经形态架构。本章将深入研究各种类型的 eNVM 设备，例如电阻式 RAM (ReRAM)、相变存储器 (PCM)、基于自旋电子学的存储设备及其在神经形态计算中的应用，讨论它们的挑战和机遇，并重点介绍它们在神经形态系统中集成的最新进展。

English Abstract

This chapter explores the critical role of emerging non-volatile memory (eNVM) devices in advancing neuromorphic computing. Inspired by the human brain's remarkable ability to perform complex tasks like pattern recognition and adaptation, neuromorphic computing offers immense potential for implementing complex neural networks (NNs) in real hardware. Despite ongoing research into the brain's complexities, its fundamental operation relies on neurons (processing units communicating via electrochemical signals) and synapses (junctions between neurons). The modifiable strength of the synapse is key to learning and memory functionality, allowing the brain to adapt and rewire itself. For dense and low-power circuits in neuromorphic computing, these eNVM devices are ideal due to their synaptic-like behavior and non-volatile information storage. Properties such as conductance modulation of these devices emulate synaptic plasticity, which is crucial for learning, and their scalability makes them promising for large-scale neuromorphic architectures. This chapter will delve into the various types of eNVM devices such as resistive RAM (ReRAM), phase-change memory (PCM), spintronics-based memory devices, and their applications in neuromorphic computing, discussing their challenges and opportunities, and highlighting recent advances in their integration within neuromorphic systems.

Going deep, reaching further: What can Boltzmann machines efficiently represent?#

作者：Tobias Reinhart、Gemma De les Coves。arXiv · cs.LG，2026-10-30
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配3

📄 摘要：While universal approximation theorems theoretically underpin the representation capabilities of machine learning models, they have two shortcomings: They do not focus on efficient representations and they tend to be ins…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

While universal approximation theorems theoretically underpin the representation capabilities of machine learning models, they have two shortcomings: They do not focus on efficient representations and they tend to be insensitive to depth and other heuristically important properties. In this work we show that these two shortcomings are intertwined. We prove that machine learning models which are deeper or have higher-order interactions can efficiently represent a larger set of functions, i.e. have larger efficient reach. It follows that deep Boltzmann machines (DBMs) have maximal efficient reach, restricted Boltzmann machines (RBMs) sub-maximal, and k-body generalizations of RBMs form a hierarchy between the two, even though all are universal. More generally, (i) we provide a full characterization of machine learning models with maximal efficient reach; (ii) we derive separations between deep and shallow models for three representation tasks; and (iii) we prove that the efficient reach of shallow models constitutes an infinite hierarchy indexed by the interaction order. The proofs draw on different methodologies, namely on (i) a recent characterization of universal spin models; (ii) computational complexity bounds for spin models; and (iii) communication complexity bounds applied to shallow models. This work shows that universal machine learning models can strongly differ in their efficient representation capabilities, and provides a theoretical foundation for heuristic observations.

PAPERBENCHX: Can AI Reproduce Science Across Disciplines?#

作者：Ruoyu Wu、Zengji Tu、Aolong Sun、Tianyi Ma、Junren Li、Xinbo Xu 等 17 人。arXiv · cs.AI，2026-10-30
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配3

📄 摘要：Before AI can be trusted to discover new science, it should be able to reproduce what science already knows. Reproduction provides a rigorous checkpoint for an AI scientist: the target claim is known, yet recovering it s…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Before AI can be trusted to discover new science, it should be able to reproduce what science already knows. Reproduction provides a rigorous checkpoint for an AI scientist: the target claim is known, yet recovering it still requires translating an incomplete scientific description into an executable experiment and producing evidence that can be independently verified. Existing research-reproduction benchmarks largely focus on AI and machine learning, where experiments share relatively homogeneous software stacks. Extending this capability across scientific disciplines introduces a different challenge: agents must operate domain-specific scientific software, make physical and numerical choices, diagnose long-running computations, and distinguish valid evidence from merely plausible outputs. We introduce PAPERBENCHX, a benchmark of 93 expert-constructed paper-reproduction tasks spanning 12 research areas across electromagnetics, photonics, chemistry, materials science, biology, and robotics. Each task provides the source paper, a public task specification, and a domain-native scientific environment involving specialized scientific software. Agents must reconstruct a key experiment, execute and diagnose it, and deliver a runnable workflow that supports the target scientific claim. To evaluate scientific validity rather than surface-level agreement, we replay every submission from scratch and score only regenerated evidence against expert-validated criteria. Across 10 frontier agent configurations, the best configuration fully reproduces only 14% tasks under the 90% threshold. Meanwhile, mean rubric scores range from 27.53% to 59.08%, indicating that substantial partial progress rarely translates into complete scientific reproduction. These results reveal a large gap between paper understanding and reliably producing reproducible scientific evidence.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

具有统计角 θin(0,π) 的任意子理想气体的基态是一个长期存在的开放问题，最近对分数陈绝缘体的观察重新引起了人们的兴趣。在这项工作中，我们构建了基于精确任意子统计的变分波函数，并将理论指导的复合玻色子、复合费米子和部分子波函数与球体上的神经网络相关性相结合。对于无自旋和 SU(2) 半子，我们确认了配对的任意子超流体，远低于最佳晶体状态。在 θ=π/3 时，无自旋情况下液体和晶体的能量更接近。对于具有 SU(3) 风格的 θ=π/3 任意子，我们找到了两个候选者。一种是斯格明子霍尔晶体，其中任意子在自旋织构的有效磁场中形成整数量子霍尔态。另一个是六任意子团簇的手性彩色超导体，是具有SU(3)色规对称性和SU(3)风味对称性的完整金属的色味锁定配对后代；它的波函数是超行列式而不是部分子行列式的乘积。在我们的变分拟构中，彩色超导体比晶体和具有 U(1)² 和 Z₃ 规范组的色味锁定相具有更好的能量。对于扭曲的MoTe2，这表明可重入整数量子反常霍尔相是掺杂任意子的斯格明子霍尔晶体，并且观察到的超导性可能是彩色超导体，其可以转变为f+if BCS超导体，手性中心电荷跳跃3。

English Abstract

The ground state of an ideal gas of anyons with statistical angle θin(0,π) is a long-standing open problem, with renewed interest from the recent observations of fractional Chern insulators. In this work we construct variational wavefunctions that build in the exact anyon statistics and combine theory-guided composite-boson, composite-fermion and parton wavefunctions with neural-network correlations on the sphere. For spinless and SU(2) semions we confirm the paired anyon superfluid, well below the best crystal states. At θ=π/3 liquids and crystals are closer in energy for the spinless case. For θ=π/3 anyons with SU(3) flavor, we find two candidates. One is a skyrmion Hall crystal, in which the anyons form an integer quantum Hall state in the effective magnetic field from the spin texture. The other is a chiral color superconductor of six-anyon clusters, the color-flavor-locked pairing descendant of a holon metal with SU(3) color gauge symmetry and SU(3) flavor symmetry; its wavefunction is a hyperdeterminant rather than a product of parton determinants. Within our variational ansatz the color superconductor has better energy than both crystals and the color-flavor locked phases with U(1)² and Z₃ gauge groups. For twisted MoTe₂ this suggests that the reentrant integer quantum anomalous Hall phase is the skyrmion Hall crystal of the doped anyons, and that the observed superconductivity may be the color superconductor, which can undergo a transition to an f+if BCS superconductor with a jump of the chiral central charge by 3.

通过量子霍尔铁磁体异质结的磁振子传输探测自旋顺序#

原题：Probing spin order via magnon transmission across quantum Hall ferromagnet heterojunctions
作者：Seung Hwan Lee、Shaowen Chen、Andrew T. Pierce、Patrick R. Forrester、Kenji Watanabe、Takashi Taniguchi 等 7 人。Nature Communications，2026-10-29
⚡ 磁性/铁电
AI×科学交叉6方向匹配0

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：量子霍尔铁磁体中的磁振子可用作非常灵敏的磁序探针。到目前为止，该技术仅应用于单一材料系统。在这里，Lee 等人将其扩展到异质结构，使用单层石墨烯中产生的磁振子来检测双层石墨烯中的自旋顺序。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

量子霍尔铁磁体中的磁振子可用作非常灵敏的磁序探针。到目前为止，该技术仅应用于单一材料系统。在这里，Lee 等人将其扩展到异质结构，使用单层石墨烯中产生的磁振子来检测双层石墨烯中的自旋顺序。

English Abstract

Two-dimensional material platforms host a remarkable array of exotic correlated phases. Probing magnetic order in these systems is essential for understanding their underlying physics, yet dilute spin densities render conventional magnetic probes ineffective. Spin waves, or magnons, in quantum Hall ferromagnets (QHFM) have proven effective for probing the magnetic order in various symmetry-broken quantum Hall (QH) phases in graphene systems, but previous works have been limited to homojunction configurations within a single material. Here, we demonstrate magnon transmission across a monolayer-bilayer graphene quantum Hall ferromagnet heterojunction—the first magnon transmission across quantum Hall ferromagnet heterojunctions, using one material as a magnon source to probe magnetic order in a distinct material. Generating magnons in monolayer graphene (MLG) atνMLG= 1, we detect their transmission through bilayer graphene (BLG) via nonlocal voltage measurements, revealing spin order in BLG symmetry-broken quantum Hall states. The transmission exhibits hallmark magnon signatures: a sharp onset at the Zeeman energy and systematic variation with Landau level filling, including suppression atνBLG= 4 and 8 where spin polarization vanishes. Our findings establish heterojunction magnon transmission as a powerful, modular probe of magnetic order, opening new avenues for investigating exotic quantum states across the rapidly expanding family of two-dimensional materials.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism、density functional theory、monte carlo simulation、electronic structure。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

完全补偿的亚铁磁体具有零温度下几乎消失的磁矩、完美的自旋极化电子结构和超快自旋动力学，代表了下一代自旋电子器件的非常有前途的材料平台。通过最大限度地减少杂散磁场和降低开关电流，FCF 为高速、低功耗自旋电子器件开辟了一条可行的道路。在这项工作中，我们对磁隧道结中 Heusler 型全补偿半金属亚铁磁体 Mn3Al 的电子结构、有限温度磁性和自旋相关输运特性进行了系统研究。我们证明了 Mn3Al 是一种半金属 FCF，在 0 K 下具有 100% 自旋极化和完全补偿的磁化强度。将密度泛函理论与蒙特卡罗模拟相结合，我们表征了其有限温度磁特性，并表明 Mn3Al 在 300 K 时表现出低于 11.6 emu/cc 的净磁化强度，证实了其近补偿亚铁磁序在室温下的鲁棒性。此外，我们预测 Mn3Al/MgO/Mn3Al MTJ 在 0 K 时产生巨大的隧道磁阻效应，在 300 K 时仍高达 232%。我们的结果表明 Mn3Al 和相关的半金属 FCF 是基于磁阻的自旋电子应用的优秀候选者。

English Abstract

Fully compensated ferrimagnets, characterized by nearly vanishing magnetic moments at zero temperature, perfect spin-polarized electronic structures, and ultrafast spin dynamics, represent a highly promising materials platform for next-generation spintronic devices. By minimizing stray magnetic fields and reducing switching currents, FCFs enable a viable path toward high-speed, low-power spintronic devices. In this work, we present a systematic investigation of the electronic structure, finite-temperature magnetism, and spin-dependent transport properties of the Heusler-type fully compensated half-metallic ferrimagnet Mn3Al in magnetic tunnel junctions. We demonstrate that Mn3Al is a half-metallic FCF with 100% spin polarization and fully compensated magnetization at 0 K. Combining density functional theory with Monte Carlo simulations, we characterize its finite-temperature magnetic properties and show that Mn3Al exhibits a net magnetization below 11.6 emu/cc at 300 K, confirming the robustness of its nearly compensated ferrimagnetic order at room temperature. Furthermore, we predict that the Mn3Al/MgO/Mn3Al MTJ yields a giant tunneling magnetoresistance effect at 0 K, which remains as high as 232% at 300 K. Our results establish Mn3Al and related half-metallic FCFs as excellent candidates for magnetoresistance-based spintronic applications.
