🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：graph neural networks、interatomic potential、molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

旋转等变架构提高了基于图神经网络 (GNN) 的机器学习 (ML) 通用材料模型的物理保真度。然而，它们的可扩展性受到训练和推理过程中使用重要的高精度张量运算的限制。采用矩阵乘法单元 (MMU) 的精度降低格式（例如，用 FP8 代替 FP32）可大幅提高吞吐量，在 NVIDIA Hopper (Blackwell) GPU 上高达 30 倍 (60 倍)，但可能会导致精度大幅下降。在这里，我们在整数 Ozaki 方案下引入了定制的“模拟”低精度运算，以加速 GNN 的“SO(2) 等变”处理层，该处理层主导了 (1) 许多最先进的 ML 原子间势 (MLIP) 和 (2) 最通用的 ML 哈密顿 (MLH) 模型的运行时间。我们的框架 FlashSO(2) 在吞吐量、准确性和内存占用方面均优于 TF32（最准确的本机低精度 MMU 格式）。在 SO(2) 层级别，它达到 MALOQ (UMA) 参考实现（MLH (MLIP) 模型）吞吐量的 40 倍 (7 倍)，在 NVIDIA GH200 上维持高达 236 (232) 有效 Tflop/s，是 FP32 峰值的 3.5 倍。在模型级别，FlashSO(2) 帮助训练 MALOQ 的速度比纯 FP32 版本快 3.9 倍，同时将峰值内存减少 58%，且准确度没有损失。在使用 UMA 的分子动力学模拟中，加速因子为 3.3 倍，峰值内存减少了 51%。因此，FlashSO(2) 将等变材料模型的速度精度帕累托前沿推向了原生低精度格式之外，并大大降低了利用其缩放定律所需的资源。

English Abstract

Rotationally equivariant architectures improve the physical fidelity of machine-learned (ML) universal materials models based on graph neural networks (GNNs). However, their scalability is limited by the use of non-trivial, high-precision tensor operations during both training and inference. Adopting the reduced-precision formats of matrix multiplication units (MMUs), e.g., FP8 instead of FP32, provides massive increases in throughput, up to 30x (60x) on NVIDIA Hopper (Blackwell) GPUs, but may lead to drastic loss of accuracy. Here, we introduce tailored 'emulated' low-precision operations under the integer Ozaki scheme to accelerate the 'SO(2)-equivariant' processing layers of GNNs, which dominate the runtime of (1) many state-of-the-art ML interatomic potentials (MLIPs) and (2) most universal ML Hamiltonian (MLH) models. Our framework, FlashSO(2), outperforms TF32, the most accurate native low-precision MMU format, in throughput, accuracy, and memory footprint at once. At the SO(2) layer level, it reaches 40x (7x) the throughput of MALOQ's (UMA's) reference implementation, an MLH (MLIP) model, sustaining up to 236 (232) effective Tflop/s on an NVIDIA GH200, 3.5x the FP32 peak. At the model level, FlashSO(2) helps train MALOQ 3.9x faster than its pure-FP32 version while reducing peak memory by 58% at no loss in accuracy. In molecular dynamics simulations with UMA, the speedup factor is 3.3x, and the peak memory is reduced by 51%. FlashSO(2) thus pushes the speed-accuracy Pareto frontier of equivariant materials models beyond the native low-precision formats and substantially lowers the resources required to exploit their scaling laws.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

电子大正则密度泛函理论捕捉了结构和电子电荷如何响应电极电位，以及与统计力学一起，这些响应如何引起涌现现象。然而，其计算成本限制了第一原理模拟可实现的系统规模和采样时间。在这里，我们引入了一种可微架构，该架构表示巨大的势能并获得力和电子电荷作为其导数，完全满足热力学一致性所需的混合导数约束，并将其在等变MACE框架内实现为GC-MACE。我们展示了跨多个电化学接口的更准确的联合学习，以及相对于以前的模型改进的更厚水膜的可转移性，同时能够以比从头分子动力学可实现的更大的长度和时间尺度进行恒电位模拟。凭借广泛的训练数据，该框架为电子大正则系综提供了一条获得热力学一致的基础原子间势的途径

English Abstract

Electronic grand-canonical density functional theory captures how structure and electronic charge respond to electrode potential and, together with statistical mechanics, how these responses give rise to emergent phenomena. However, its computational cost limits the system sizes and sampling times accessible to first- principles simulations. Here, we introduce a differentiable architecture that represents the grand potential and obtains forces and electronic charge as its derivatives, exactly satisfying the mixed-derivative constraints required for thermodynamic consistency, and implement it within the equivariant MACE framework as GC- MACE. We demonstrate more accurate joint learning across multiple electrochemical interfaces and improved transferability to thicker water films relative to previous models, while enabling constant-potential simulations at substantially larger length and time scales than accessible to ab initio molecular dynamics. With broad training data, the framework offers a route to thermodynamically consistent foundation interatomic potentials for the electronic grand canonical ensemble

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

晶体生成模型主要学习稳定晶体的外观，很少明确监督它们稳定的原因。我们通过能量探测揭示了最先进的晶体生成模型与预训练的通用机器学习原子间势（MLIP）之间存在巨大的表征差距，并表明这种差距可以通过简单的训练时间对齐来弥补。我们提出了 Crystal REPresentation Alignment (CrystalREPA)，这是一个即插即用的框架，它通过元素感知对比目标将生成编码器的原子隐藏状态与冻结的 MLIP 表示对齐，以边际训练开销传输稳定性感知原子先验，并且无需额外的推理成本。通过三个生成框架、十名 MLIP 教师和两个基准数据集，CrystalREPA 不断提高生成晶体的热力学稳定性、结构有效性和结构保真度。同样重要的是，我们发现 MLIP 的迁移有效性很难通过其在标准排行榜（例如 Matbench Discovery）上的准确性来预测，但可以通过其原子表示空间的可区分性来强烈预测，从而产生了一个实用的、与准确性无关的标准来选择 MLIP 教师进行生成迁移。代码可在 https://github.com/Chengqian-Zhang/CrystalREPA 获取。

English Abstract

Crystal generative models mainly learn what stable crystals look like, with little explicit supervision for what makes them stable. We reveal a substantial representation gap between state-of-the-art crystal generative models and pretrained universal machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) via energy probing, and show this gap can be closed by a simple training-time alignment. We propose Crystal REPresentation Alignment (CrystalREPA), a plug-and-play framework that aligns the atom-wise hidden states of generative encoders with frozen MLIP representations through an element-aware contrastive objective, transferring stability-aware atomistic priors with marginal training overhead and no additional inference cost. Across three generative frameworks, ten MLIP teachers, and two benchmark datasets, CrystalREPA consistently improves the thermodynamic stability, structural validity, and structural fidelity of generated crystals. Equally important, we find that an MLIP's transfer effectiveness is poorly predicted by its accuracy on standard leaderboards (e.g., Matbench Discovery) but strongly predicted by the distinguishability of its atom-wise representation space, yielding a practical, accuracy-independent criterion for selecting MLIP teachers for generative transfer. Code is available at https://github.com/Chengqian-Zhang/CrystalREPA.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

电子圆二色性 (ECD) 是分配手性分子绝对构型的关键实验探针，但解释测量的光谱通常需要昂贵的时间相关密度泛函理论 (TDDFT) 计算。现有的几种用于 ECD 预测的机器学习方法速度更快，但其准确性仍然远远低于实验应用所需的水平。在这里，我们介绍 PhysECD，这是一种奇偶校验 E(3) 等变框架，它从其基础量子化学量构建光谱，通过构建确保正确的手性光学对称性。为了提高泛化性并支持实验应用，我们构建了 SCECD，这是一个由 578,104 个计算光谱组成的构象解析数据集，涵盖具有 1 到 5 个立构中心的多种天然产物。 PhysECD 可在几毫秒内根据构象异构体几何形状预测 ECD 光谱，从而在 CMCDS 基准和 SCECD 上实现最先进的精度。针对 77 个实验光谱的验证进一步表明，PhysECD 可以作为 TDDFT 的快速替代方案，用于指定复杂手性分子的绝对构型。

English Abstract

Electronic circular dichroism (ECD) is a key experimental probe for assigning the absolute configuration of chiral molecules, but interpreting measured spectra often requires costly time-dependent density functional theory (TDDFT) calculations. The few existing machine learning methods for ECD prediction are faster, but their accuracy still falls well short of that required for experimental applications. Here we introduce PhysECD, a parity-aware E(3)-equivariant framework that builds the spectrum from its underlying quantum-chemical quantities, ensuring correct chiroptical symmetry by construction. To improve generalization and support experimental applications, we construct SCECD, a conformer-resolved dataset of 578,104 computed spectra spanning diverse natural products with one to five stereocentres. PhysECD predicts ECD spectra from conformer geometries in milliseconds, achieving state-of-the-art accuracy on the CMCDS benchmark and SCECD. Validation against 77 experimental spectra further demonstrates that PhysECD can serve as a fast alternative to TDDFT for assigning the absolute configuration of complex chiral molecules.

通过混合 HiPIMS/脉冲直流 PVD 工艺机器学习辅助设计复杂高熵合金，用于极端环境中的低碳能源应用#

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

复杂和高熵合金由于其在恶劣环境下的机械和耐腐蚀性能而备受关注，特别是无碳能源应用所需的性能。然而，由于其复杂性和鸡尾酒效应，通过试错方法以散装和薄膜形式进行加工是不切实际的。人工智能的最新发展为其属性的阐述和调整带来了新的可能性。首先，我们概述材料和数据科学研究。然后，我们描述了 DIADEM（法国材料和数据科学融合倡议）如何通过开发全国性的合成和表征平台网络（DIADEM 发现中心）来解决无碳能源应用（核、高温电解等）创新涂层的开发问题。我们特别描述了 DIADEM-2D，这是一种人工智能驱动的混合 HiPIMS/脉冲 DC PVD 工艺，使用共焦组合配置的 4 个阴极。我们使用文献中的数据来确定高熵合金在熔盐介质和核事故条件下的耐腐蚀性。已经实现了收集沉积参数和涂层特性的独立于元素的模型，允许设计具有特定成分的保护涂层。我们证明了这个过程的可行性及其准确性。

English Abstract

Complex and high entropy alloys are attracting much attention currently thanks to their mechanical and corrosion resistance properties in harsh environments, in particular needed for carbon-free energy applications. However, their elaboration in bulk and in thin film form in a trial-and-error approach is impractical due to their complexity and the cocktail effect. The recent development of artificial intelligence brings a new possibility for their elaboration and adjustment of their properties. Firstly, we present an overview of Materials and data science research. Then we describe how DIADEM - French initiative for Materials and Data science convergence - tackles the development of innovative coatings for carbon-free energy applications (nuclear, high temperature electrolysis, ...) thanks to the development of a nationwide network of synthesis and characterization platforms - the DIADEM discovery hub. We describe in particular DIADEM-2D, an AI-driven Hybrid HiPIMS/Pulsed-DC PVD process using 4 cathodes in confocal combinatorial configuration. We present the high entropy alloy determination using data from the literature for corrosion resistance in molten salt media and nuclear accidental conditions. An element-independent model gathering deposition parameters and coating properties has been implemented allowing the design of protective coatings with a particular composition. We demonstrate the feasibility of this process and its accuracy.

机器学习支持考虑现实世界限制的可持续合金设计#

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

设计可持续合金必须协调高性能与现实世界的限制，包括原料稀缺性和可变性、加工和能源要求、监管阈值和经济限制。解决这些限制远远超出了当今传统的以性能为中心的框架的范围。它不仅需要考虑复杂的财产-加工-性能权衡，还需要考虑可持续的生产途径。在这里，我们提出了一种物理信息集成（PIE）学习框架，它将基本结构-性能关系与基于机制的合金设计的加工路线和可持续性标准相结合。它根据成分-微观结构关系预测性能，并推断可持续性约束下的加工路线，建立跨学科方法来识别可持续合金解决方案。我们验证了镁合金（一种轻质但能源密集型金属材料类别）的方法，同时考虑了性能、成本和碳排放。 PIE 模型在基准数据集上进行训练，系统地探索合理的合金成分和微观结构，以构建包含数百万种候选合金的数字库。从这个领域，我们确定了两种可持续的镁合金解决方案，克服了传统的性能-成本-排放权衡：与成本和排放相当的同类合金相比，含稀土合金的产量和抗拉强度分别提高了 72.8% 和 61.1%；与主要原料相比，不含稀土的合金可分别降低成本和碳排放 40.4% 和 88.9%，且不会影响机械性能。这项工作介绍了一个通用的、基于物理的、可量化的框架，用于在现实世界的限制下设计可持续合金。

English Abstract

Designing sustainable alloys must reconcile high performance with real-world constraints, including feedstock scarcity and variability, processing and energy requirements, regulatory thresholds, and economic limitations. Addressing these constraints reaches far beyond today's conventional performance-focused frameworks. It needs to consider not only complex property-processing-performance trade-offs but also sustainable production pathways. Here, we propose a physics-informed ensemble (PIE) learning framework that integrates fundamental structure-property relationships with processing routes and sustainability criteria for mechanism-based alloy design. It predicts properties from composition-microstructure relationships and infers processing routes under sustainability constraints, establishing an interdisciplinary approach to identifying sustainable alloy solutions. We validate the approach for Mg alloys, a lightweight yet energy-intensive metallic material class, considering performance, cost, and carbon emissions. Trained on a benchmark dataset, the PIE model systematically explores plausible alloy compositions and microstructures to construct a digital library of millions of candidate alloys. From this space, we identify two sustainable Mg alloy solutions that overcome the conventional performance-cost-emission trade-off: a rare-earth-containing alloy increases yield and tensile strength by 72.8 percent and 61.1 percent, respectively, over alloys from the same family at comparable cost and emissions; and a rare-earth-free alloy reduces cost and carbon emissions by 40.4 percent and 88.9 percent, respectively, relative to its primary-feedstock counterpart, without compromising mechanical performance. This work introduces a general, physics-informed, and quantifiable framework for designing sustainable alloys under real-world constraints.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

锂离子电池广泛应用于工业应用，但其性能会随着时间的推移而逐渐下降，因此准确的寿命预测对于安全性和可靠性非常重要。然而，可靠的寿命评估通常需要大量的降解测试，这既昂贵又耗时。为了减轻这种负担，最近的数据驱动方法试图仅从最初的几个周期来预测电池的寿命周期。然而，从如此有限的早期周期数据中识别可靠的降解模式仍然具有挑战性，因为早期老化动态通常是微妙的和非线性的。为了解决这个问题，我们采用连续小波变换将周期电压、电流和容量信号转换为时频表示，从而实现退化行为的多分辨率表征。在此表示的基础上，我们应用尺度压缩来减少冗余，同时保留尺度图中的结构信息。我们在八个基准数据集上评估了所提出的框架，这些基准数据集源自七个公共电池数据集，涵盖不同的电极化学成分、电池形状因素和循环协议。实验结果表明，所提出的框架在单周期剩余使用寿命预测方面始终优于现有方法，并且随着输入周期数从 1 增加到 100，仍然保持鲁棒性。此外，冗余保真度分析表征了尺度压缩引入的权衡，基于梯度的视觉解释表明该模型侧重于与充放电操作期间结构化信号转换相关的局部时频区域。这些结果证明了所提出的早期电池剩余使用寿命预测框架的有效性和可解释性。

English Abstract

Lithium-ion batteries are widely used in industrial applications, but their performance gradually degrades over time, making accurate lifetime prediction important for safety and reliability. However, reliable lifetime assessment generally requires extensive degradation testing, which is both costly and time-consuming. To reduce this burden, recent data-driven approaches have sought to predict the end-of-life cycle of batteries from only the initial few cycles. Nevertheless, identifying reliable degradation patterns from such limited early-cycle data remains challenging because early aging dynamics are often subtle and nonlinear. To address this issue, we adopt the continuous wavelet transform to convert cycle-wise voltage, current, and capacity signals into time-frequency representations, enabling multi-resolution characterization of degradation behavior. Building on this representation, we apply scale compression to reduce redundancy while preserving structural information in the scalograms. We evaluate the proposed framework on eight benchmark datasets derived from seven public battery datasets covering diverse electrode chemistries, cell form factors, and cycling protocols. Experimental results show that the proposed framework consistently outperforms prior approaches in single-cycle remaining useful life prediction and remains robust as the number of input cycles increases from 1 to 100. In addition, redundancy-fidelity analysis characterizes the trade-off introduced by scale compression, and gradient-based visual explanations indicate that the model focuses on localized time-frequency regions associated with structured signal transitions during charge-discharge operation. These results demonstrate the effectiveness and interpretability of the proposed framework for early-cycle battery remaining useful life prediction.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

传统密度泛函近似的缺点阻碍了密度泛函理论在多相催化中的应用。我们将机器学习与明确的非本地物理通知描述符相结合，并引入了一个交换相关函数（CIDER26SS）框架，该框架经过正规化以实现广泛的可转移性。 CIDER26SS 具有尺寸广泛、高效的特点，可对分子和固态系统提供平衡且准确的描述，并且专门针对过渡金属表面化学进行了良好优化。 CIDER26SS 超越了现有的传统泛函，解决了 CO/Pt 难题，识别了 Pt(111) 表面上 CO 吸附的正确结合位点，以及准确的吸附能、Pt 晶格常数和表面能。即使 Pt 的所有本体和表面数据均被排除在训练集中，预测结果与实验值也非常吻合。值得注意的是，即使对于远离训练域的系统，CIDER26SS 也超过了半局部近似的精度。

English Abstract

The application of density functional theory to heterogeneous catalysis is hindered by the shortcomings of conventional density functional approximations. We combine machine learning with explicitly non-local physically informed descriptors and introduce an exchange-correlation functional (CIDER26SS) framework regularized for wide transferability. CIDER26SS is size-extensive, highly efficient, provides a balanced and accurate description of both molecular and solid-state systems, and is specifically well-optimized for transition metal surface chemistry. Surpassing existing conventional functionals, CIDER26SS resolves the CO/Pt puzzle, identifying the correct binding site for CO adsorption on the Pt(111) surface, along with an accurate adsorption energy, Pt lattice constant, and surface energy. Predictions agree well with the experimental values, even when all bulk and surface data for Pt are excluded from the training set. Remarkably, CIDER26SS exceeds the accuracy of semilocal approximations even for systems far outside the training domain.

AtomSteer：原子生成模型的测试时间约束引导#

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：crystal structure prediction。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

扩散和流动匹配模型提供了可扩展的方法来生成晶体材料、分子构象异构体和蛋白质-配体复合物。然而，满足晶体学对称性、结构几何形状和精确的立体化学仍然具有挑战性。满足这些要求需要进行修正，以保留预训练模型已捕获的几何形状。在这项工作中，我们提出了 AtomSteer，这是一种测试时推理框架，用于引导具有结构约束的预训练原子生成模型。在采样过程中，AtomSteer 使用有界局部投影、图刚性立体化学变换和周期性对称性投影来校正中间结构预测，接受可提高约束满足度的更新，同时限制对结构其余部分的更改。我们展示了 AtomSteer 跨三个预训练主干 FlowMM、ET-Flow 和 Boltz-2，涵盖从头晶体生成和晶体结构预测、分子构象异构体生成和蛋白质-配体复合物结构预测。 AtomSteer 改善了从头晶体生成中的空间群分布覆盖范围，并改善了晶体结构预测中的目标空间群恢复。 AtomSteer 提高了分子有效性和 E/Z 立体化学准确性，同时保持构象异构体整体保真度。对于蛋白质-配体复合物，AtomSteer 减少了相对于实验参考结构的配体 E/Z 误差，同时实现了竞争性对接成功和 PoseBusters 有效性。我们的方法提供了一个统一的框架，可以将化学和晶体学控制添加到现有的原子生成模型中，而无需重新训练或架构更改。

English Abstract

Diffusion and flow matching models offer scalable approaches to generating crystalline materials, molecular conformers, and protein-ligand complexes. However, satisfying crystallographic symmetry, structural geometry, and accurate stereochemistry remains challenging. Satisfying these requirements calls for corrections that preserve the geometry already captured by the pretrained model. In this work, we propose AtomSteer, a test-time inference framework for steering pretrained atomistic generative models with structural constraints. During sampling, AtomSteer corrects intermediate structure predictions with bounded local projections, graph-rigid stereochemical transformations, and projections over periodic symmetries, accepting updates that improve constraint satisfaction while limiting changes to the rest of the structure. We demonstrate AtomSteer across three pretrained backbones, FlowMM, ET-Flow, and Boltz-2, spanning de novo crystal generation and crystal structure prediction, molecular conformer generation, and protein-ligand complex structure prediction. AtomSteer improves space-group distribution coverage in de novo crystal generation and improves target space-group recovery in crystal structure prediction. AtomSteer improves molecular validity and E/Z stereochemical accuracy while retaining conformer ensemble fidelity. For protein-ligand complexes, AtomSteer reduces ligand E/Z errors relative to experimental reference structures while achieving competitive docking success and PoseBusters validity. Our method provides a unified framework for adding chemical and crystallographic control to existing atomistic generative models without retraining or architectural changes.

室温、常压超导体候选物的人工智能引导计算设计：Grokene#

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们介绍了 Grokene，一种源自石墨烯的新型二维超晶格，它是通过利用大型语言模型的人工智能引导材料发现工作流程来识别的。 Grokene 预计将表现出环境压力、室温超导性，计算模拟显示较高的电子声子耦合常数和相当大的对数平均声子频率（约 1650 K），导致平均场临界温度约为 325 K。完全各向同性的 Eliashberg 解决方案进一步支持 310 K 左右的临界温度，强调了其室温超导的强大潜力。然而，Grokene 严格的二维性质引入了相位波动，将可观测的超导转变限制在单层中约 120 K 的 Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless (BKT) 温度。为了将 TBKT 提升至室温，提出了诸如少层堆叠、衬底或栅极工程以及超晶格结构和掺杂水平优化等策略。我们的集成工作流程将人工智能驱动的材料发现与先进的多体理论（DFPT/EPW、Eliashberg 和 RPA）相结合，为探索新型超导体提供了系统且可重复的框架。我们认为，Grokene 的实验合成和综合表征对于评估这些计算预测并探索在环境压力下实现实用超导的途径至关重要。

English Abstract

We introduce Grokene, a novel two-dimensional superlattice derived from graphene, which was identified through an AI-guided materials discovery workflow utilizing a large language model. Grokene is predicted to exhibit ambient-pressure, room-temperature superconductivity, with computational simulations revealing a high electron-phonon coupling constant and a substantial logarithmic-averaged phonon frequency (~1650 K), leading to a mean-field critical temperature of approximately 325 K. Full isotropic Eliashberg solutions further support a critical temperature around 310 K, underscoring its strong potential for room-temperature superconductivity. However, the strict two-dimensional nature of Grokene introduces phase fluctuations, limiting the observable superconducting transition to a Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless (BKT) temperature of about 120 K in monolayers. To elevate TBKT toward room temperature, strategies such as few-layer stacking, substrate or gate engineering, and optimization of superlattice structure and doping levels are proposed. Our integrated workflow, combining AI-driven materials discovery with advanced many-body theories (DFPT/EPW, Eliashberg, and RPA), provides a systematic and reproducible framework for exploring novel superconductors. We suggest that experimental synthesis and comprehensive characterization of Grokene will be essential to assess these computational predictions and to explore routes toward practical superconductivity under ambient pressure.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

从瞬态响应数据推断本构定律是软凝聚态物理中的一个中心反问题，特别是当相关弛豫谱超出单个实验的时间窗口时。时间-温度叠加 (TTS) 通过允许温度重新调整特征弛豫时间而不改变弛豫谱的基本形状，从而限制了线性粘弹性区域中热流变简单材料的这个问题。我们将此约束纳入基于物理的机器学习框架中，通过线性调频流变测量实现时间分辨机械光谱。我们将紧凑分数粘弹性模型编码为源自玻尔兹曼叠加原理的因果卷积特征，并使用稀疏变分高斯过程回归联合拟合多温度线性调频记录。该模型直接从线性粘弹性区域中测量的时域应力应变信号推断水平位移因子、本构参数和观测噪声尺度，无需首先提取频域谱或手动移动单独的曲线。对于合成分数麦克斯韦数据，该方法从规定的模型库中恢复生成本构定律，并在应力噪声水平上恢复 3% 至 25% 的潜在变化。应用于跨越玻璃化转变体系的 DGEBA-IPDA 环氧树脂数据集，合并似然使用贝叶斯信息标准区分竞争的分数流变模型。这些结果将 TTS 主曲线构造重新构建为对潜伏时间重新缩放和线性粘弹性的概率推断，同时还确定了局限性，例如同方差噪声和信息量较弱的光谱范围。

English Abstract

Inferring constitutive laws from transient response data is a central inverse problem in soft condensed-matter physics, particularly when the relevant relaxation spectrum extends beyond the time window of a single experiment. Time-temperature superposition (TTS) constrains this problem for thermorheologically simple materials in the linear viscoelastic regime by allowing temperature to rescale the characteristic relaxation time without changing the underlying shape of the relaxation spectrum. We incorporate this constraint into a physics-informed machine-learning framework for time-resolved mechanical spectroscopy via chirp rheometry. We encode compact fractional viscoelastic models as causal convolutional features derived from the Boltzmann superposition principle, and we jointly fit multi-temperature chirp records using sparse variational Gaussian process regression. The model infers horizontal shift factors, constitutive parameters, and an observational-noise scale directly from time-domain stress-strain signals measured in the linear viscoelastic regime, without first extracting frequency-domain spectra or manually shifting separate curves. For synthetic Fractional Maxwell data, the method recovers the generating constitutive law from a prescribed model library and latent shifts over stress-noise levels from 3% to 25%. Applied to a DGEBA-IPDA epoxy dataset spanning the glass-transition regime, the pooled likelihood distinguishes competing fractional rheology models using the Bayesian information criterion. These results recast TTS master-curve construction as probabilistic inference over latent time rescaling and linear viscoelasticity, while also identifying limitations, such as homoscedastic noise and weakly informative spectral regimes.

基于物理知识图的安全强化学习，具有特定于约束的批评，用于配电网络中的社区电池协调#

中文摘要

分布式能源 (DER) 的高渗透率和快速变化的电动汽车 (EV) 充电加剧了配电网络 (DN) 中的电压和线路限制违规行为，使安全、低成本运营变得越来越具有挑战性。本文提出了一种基于物理的安全深度强化学习（DRL）框架，用于协调社区电池储能系统，以减轻约束违规，同时最大限度地降低运营成本。为了表示非局域时空相互作用，我们开发了一种双层物理信息图形表示，其中线路阻抗和容量塑造空间信息传播，而功率平衡、电压分布和流量重建损耗将潜在状态规范化，以实现与控制相关的物理一致性。基于这种表示，我们开发了拉格朗日专用批评家 PPO (LDCPPO)，它实现了特定于约束的价值分解：独立的奖励和成本批评家保留与电压和线路负载相关的异构学习信号，并仅在拉格朗日策略更新期间结合它们的优势。针对屋顶光伏渗透率高的DN和电动汽车充电的案例研究表明，与测试基线相比，该方法将关键电压违规程度降低了80.5%，同时还实现了最低的电压违规次数和线路过载程度，并具有具有竞争力的运营成本。

English Abstract

High penetrations of distributed energy resources (DERs) and fast-varying electric vehicle (EV) charging intensify voltage and line-limit violations in distribution networks (DNs), making safe, low-cost operation increasingly challenging. This paper proposes a physics-informed safe deep reinforcement learning (DRL) framework for coordinating community battery energy storage systems to mitigate constraint violations while minimizing operating cost. To represent nonlocal spatiotemporal interactions, we develop a dual-level physics-informed graph representation in which line impedance and capacity shape spatial information propagation, while power-balance, voltage-profile, and flow-reconstruction losses regularize the latent state toward control-relevant physical consistency. Building on this representation, we develop Lagrangian Dedicated-Critic PPO (LDCPPO), which implements constraint-specific value decomposition: independent reward and cost critics preserve heterogeneous voltage- and line-loading-related learning signals and combine their advantages only during the Lagrangian policy update. Case studies on DNs with high rooftop photovoltaic penetration and EV charging demonstrate that the proposed method reduces the key voltage-violation degree by up to 80.5% compared with the tested baselines, while also achieving the lowest voltage-violation count and line-overload degree with competitive operating cost.

NEOPIC：基于粒子的动力学等离子体模拟的神经算子框架#

原题：NEOPIC: A Neural Operator Framework for Particle-based Kinetic Plasma Simulations
作者：Sriramkrishnan Muralikrishnan、Chelsea Maria John、Jeffrey Kelling、Michael Bussmann、Robert Speck、Paul Gibbon。arXiv · cond-mat.supr-con，2026-10-30

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：我们介绍 NEOPIC，这是一个用于学习应用于动力学等离子体模拟的基于粒子的方法中的场算子的框架。它的关键新颖之处在于神经算子直接作用于粒子的经验测量，而不是作用于网格，并在粒子位置进行评估。不需要网格或树，并且与基于网格的神经算子不同，网格上不会发生沉积，因此主导粒子模拟输入的粒子噪声不会混叠。由于它仅取代场求解，因此它可以作为基于粒子的方法的即插即用场求解器：它可以根据来自单元内粒子、傅立叶粒子或树代码的数据进行训练，并与任何时间积…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们介绍 NEOPIC，这是一个用于学习应用于动力学等离子体模拟的基于粒子的方法中的场算子的框架。它的关键新颖之处在于神经算子直接作用于粒子的经验测量，而不是作用于网格，并在粒子位置进行评估。不需要网格或树，并且与基于网格的神经算子不同，网格上不会发生沉积，因此主导粒子模拟输入的粒子噪声不会混叠。由于它仅取代场求解，因此它可以作为基于粒子的方法的即插即用场求解器：它可以根据来自单元内粒子、傅立叶粒子或树代码的数据进行训练，并与任何时间积分器（包括隐式方案）相结合。我们证明了离散评估与任意数量粒子上的连续算子一致（表示等价），并导出了将采样、学习和时间步长误差分开的误差分析：直到学习误差，NEOPIC 都具有标准蒙特卡罗粒子方法的误差结构。我们证明训练数据中的粒子噪声充当隐式正则化器，它为与推理中使用的粒子数相关的训练粒子数提供指导。复杂性分析表明 NEOPIC 是一种 O(Nₚ) 方法，其常数由架构单独设置，独立于任何网格或树。二维和三维静电基准的数值实验，包括朗道阻尼、双流和尾部碰撞不稳定性、回旋加速器迷你应用程序和潘宁陷阱，证实了理论发现。在由不同参考方法生成的多个迷你应用程序上训练的单个模型可以捕获所有这些模型，并在训练时间窗口的两倍内保持稳定，这指向基于粒子的方法中的领域的基础模型。

English Abstract

We introduce NEOPIC, a framework for learning the field operator in particle-based methods applied to kinetic plasma simulations. Its key novelty is a neural operator that acts directly on the empirical measure of the particles, rather than on a grid, and is evaluated at the particle positions. No mesh or tree is needed and, unlike grid-based neural operators, no deposition onto a mesh takes place, so the particle noise that dominates the inputs of particle simulations is not aliased. Since it replaces only the field solve, it serves as a plug-and-play field solver for particle-based methods: it can be trained on data from particle-in-cell, particle-in-Fourier or tree codes, and combined with any time integrator, including implicit schemes. We prove that the discrete evaluation coincides with the continuous operator at any number of particles (representation equivalence) and derive an error analysis that separates sampling, learning and time-stepping errors: up to the learning error, NEOPIC has the error structure of standard Monte Carlo particle methods. We show that the particle noise in the training data acts as an implicit regularizer, which gives guidance on the number of training particles in relation to the particle numbers used at inference. A complexity analysis shows that NEOPIC is an O(Nₚ) method whose constants are set by the architecture alone, independently of any mesh or tree. Numerical experiments on electrostatic benchmarks in two and three dimensions, including Landau damping, two-stream and bump-on-tail instabilities, a cyclotron mini-app and a Penning trap, confirm the theoretical findings. A single model trained on several mini-apps generated by different reference methods captures all of them and remains stable up to twice the training time window, which points towards foundation models for fields in particle-based methods.

机器学习潜力的对比正则化#

原题：Contrastive Regularization of Machine Learning Potentials

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习模型越来越多地被训练为回归器，然后部署为生成器，从语言模型到学习模拟器。在这种情况下，保留数据的准确性几乎不能说明模型在采样时产生的分布。我们研究这个问题，其中正确的分布是由第一原理确定的，在机器学习原子间势（MLIP）中，通过最小化密度泛函理论（DFT）能量和力的均方误差（MSE）来训练，然后用于运行分子模拟。 MSE 永远不会限制势所定义的玻尔兹曼分布，因此 MLIP 可以匹配保留的 DFT 数据，并且在采样时仍然会漂移到破坏其热力学的虚假最小值。基于统计物理学的变分原理，我们引入了对比正则化 MSE (CRMSE)，这是一个训练后步骤，其中势充当其自己的基于能量的模型，并学习重现其生成的分布，而无需任何新的 DFT 数据。对于 MD17 基准的分子，CRMSE 恢复物理采样并恢复 DFT 自由能分布，同时将能量和力误差保持在化学精度范围内。我们的结果表明，要采样的模型必须对其产生的分布进行训练，而不仅仅是为了逐点精度。

English Abstract

Machine learning models are increasingly trained as regressors and then deployed as generators, from language models to learned simulators. In this setting, accuracy on held-out data says little about the distribution a model produces when it is sampled. We study this problem where the correct distribution is fixed by first principles, in machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) trained by minimizing the mean squared error (MSE) on density functional theory (DFT) energies and forces, then used to run molecular simulations. The MSE never constrains the Boltzmann distribution that the potential defines, so an MLIP can match held-out DFT data and still drift, when sampled, into spurious minima that corrupt its thermodynamics. Building on the variational principle of statistical physics, we introduce Contrastive Regularized MSE (CRMSE), a post-training step in which the potential acts as its own energy-based model and learns to reproduce the distribution it generates, without any new DFT data. On molecules from the MD17 benchmark, CRMSE restores physical sampling and recovers DFT free-energy profiles, while keeping energy and force errors within chemical accuracy. Our results suggest that a model meant to be sampled must be trained on the distribution it produces, not only for pointwise accuracy.

金刚石表面及其与氮化硅和二氧化硅滑动界面的机器学习潜力的开发和应用：钝化、取向和负载的影响#

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

金刚石的摩擦和磨损由其表面化学控制，这决定了滑动界面上是否形成共价键。第一原理模拟探索了表面取向和钝化的作用，但仅限于几百个原子和几十皮秒。为了弥补这一差距，我们开发了 C/H/N/O/Si 化学空间的反应式机器学习原子间势，涵盖金刚石、氮化硅、氧化硅及其表面、界面和环境分子。该潜力在不同结构的大型数据集上进行了训练，并在多种物理特性上进行了测试，包括金刚石表面上的从头算反应势垒以及非晶 Si₃N₄ 和 SiO2 的结构/机械特性。然后，我们模拟具有四个表面方向、清洁和 H/OH 钝化终端以及 0.1 至 23 GPa 的法向载荷的金刚石-Si₃N₄ 界面的滑动。我们确定了两种不同的状态：一种是滑动集中在界面处，剪切应力与界面键合密度成正比，另一种是剪切力分布在较软的材料内部，并且由于完全界面键合而产生的粘附力占主导地位。这些结果表明钝化、表面取向和载荷如何共同决定界面结合和摩擦，并对金刚石磨损的环境敏感性产生影响。更广泛地说，这种潜力使得金刚石、氮化硅和二氧化硅的反应模拟能够在长度和时间尺度上以接近从头开始的精度超出第一原理方法，为未来研究犁耕粗糙度磨损、化学机械抛光和非晶陶瓷薄膜的性能提供了有用的工具。

English Abstract

Friction and wear of diamond are controlled by its surface chemistry, which determines whether covalent bonds form across the sliding interface. First-principles simulations have explored the role of surface orientation and passivation, but are limited to a few hundred atoms and tens of picoseconds. To bridge this gap, we develop a reactive machine learning interatomic potential for the C/H/N/O/Si chemical space, covering diamond, silicon nitride, silicon oxide, their surfaces, interfaces, and environmental molecules. The potential is trained on a large dataset of diverse structures and is tested on multiple physical properties, including ab initio reaction barriers on diamond surfaces, and structural/mechanical properties of amorphous Si₃N₄ and SiO₂. We then simulate sliding of diamond-Si₃N₄ interfaces with four surface orientations, clean and H/OH-passivated terminations, and normal loads from 0.1 to 23 GPa. We identify two different regimes: one where sliding is localized at the interface, and shear stress is proportional to the interfacial bond density, and another where shear is distributed inside the softer material, and adhesion due to full interfacial bonding dominates. These results show how passivation, surface orientation and load together determine interfacial bonding and friction, with implications for the environmental sensitivity of diamond wear. More broadly, the potential enables reactive simulations of diamond, silicon nitride and silica with near-ab initio accuracy at length and time scales beyond first-principles methods, providing a useful tool for future studies of wear at plowing asperities, chemical-mechanical polishing, and properties of amorphous ceramic films.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

本征堆垛层错的平衡宽度是影响关键变形现象的重要材料参数。在这里，我们对不同 Al 含量的铜铝合金使用 MD（具有经典嵌入原子势 (ZW) 和机器学习的高斯近似势 (GAP)）和密度泛函理论 (DFT) 生成的 γ 表面进行比较评估，并将它们作为能量项纳入 PFDD 模拟中以获得平衡 SFW。所有三种方法都再现了铜和铜铝合金的晶格参数和弹性模量，彼此相当一致，并且与文献中的实验测量结果一致，但 ZW 和 GAP 的 MD 导出的 γ 表面在七个特征点上与 DFT 存在很大偏差，其中在 T 和 T₁ 处差异最大。当合并到PFDD中时，DFT衍生的γ表面成功地预测了平衡SFW，该平衡SFW随着Al含量的增加而增加，并且与纯Cu的独立MD测量和实验值非常吻合，而MD衍生的γ表面在PFDD中不能产生准确的SFW，因为改变的广义堆垛层错能量景观导致解离部分在分离后很快被钉扎。这些结果表明，DFT 衍生的 γ 表面仍然是合金 SFW 预测的更可靠途径，并且原子间势必须准确捕获剪切路径，才能对合金中的堆垛层错进行稳健、高效的 MD-PFDD 建模。

English Abstract

The equilibrium width of intrinsic stacking faults is an important material parameter affecting key deformation phenomena. Here, we perform a comparative assessment of γ-surfaces generated using MD -- with a classical embedded-atom potential (ZW) and a machine-learned Gaussian Approximation Potential (GAP) -- and density functional theory (DFT), for Cu-Al alloys of varying Al content, and incorporate them as an energy term in PFDD simulations to obtain the equilibrium SFW. All three methods reproduce the lattice parameter and elastic moduli of Cu and Cu-Al alloys in reasonable agreement with each other and with experimental measurements from literature, but the MD-derived γ-surfaces, for both ZW and GAP, deviate substantially from DFT at the seven characteristic points, with the largest discrepancies at T and T₁. When incorporated into PFDD, the DFT-derived γ-surfaces successfully predict an equilibrium SFW that increases with Al content and agrees well with standalone MD measurements and experimental values for pure Cu, whereas the MD-derived γ-surfaces do not yield an accurate SFW within PFDD, as the altered generalized stacking fault energy landscape causes the dissociating partials to become pinned soon after separation. These results show that DFT-derived γ-surfaces remain the more reliable route to alloy SFW prediction, and interatomic potentials must capture the shear path accurately to enable robust, efficient MD-PFDD modelling of stacking faults in alloys.

电荷调节效应的机器学习加速模拟#

原题：Machine Learning Accelerated Simulation of Charge Regulation Effects
作者：Vijay Yadav、Sunita Kumari、Prabhat K. Jaiswal。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-26

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

许多溶剂化材料，从纳米粒子到聚电解质，通过表面基团的解离或缔合获得电荷，这种现象称为电荷调节（CR）。最近的方法使用混合电荷调节蒙特卡罗（CR-MC）/分子动力学（MD）来进化系统，同时动态调整表面基团上的电荷。然而，这种方法的计算成本很高，因为蒙特卡罗采样的成本与随附的 MD 一样高。在这项工作中，我们引入了电荷调节机器学习（CR-ML），这是一种图注意力网络，可以根据瞬时构型、表面基团的解离常数和溶液条件来预测每个表面位点的电离概率。我们证明 CR-ML 再现了疏水性聚阴离子的 CR-MC 获得的平衡构象和电离度。我们在由聚电解质介导的两个纳米粒子组成的更复杂的系统上进一步测试了 CR-ML，它准确地预测了两个粒子之间产生的电荷不对称性。在这些系统中，CR-ML 的更新速度比 CR-MC 快一个数量级，同时准确地再现其平衡特性。该框架为电荷调节材料的大规模模拟开辟了一条道路，以了解和设计其响应行为。

English Abstract

Many solvated materials, from nanoparticles to polyelectrolytes, acquire charge through the dissociation or association of surface groups, a phenomenon known as charge regulation (CR). Recent methods use hybrid charge regulation Monte Carlo (CR-MC)/molecular dynamics (MD) to evolve the system while dynamically adjusting charges on the surface groups. However, this approach is computationally expensive, as Monte Carlo sampling can be as costly as the accompanying MD. In this work, we introduce charge regulation machine learning (CR-ML), a graph attention network that predicts the ionization probability of every surface site from the instantaneous configuration, the dissociation constant of surface groups, and the solution conditions. We show that CR-ML reproduces the equilibrium conformations and degree of ionization obtained with CR-MC for a hydrophobic polyanion. We further test CR-ML on the more complex system of two nanoparticles mediated by a polyelectrolyte, where it accurately predicts the charge asymmetry that develops between the two particles. Across these systems, CR-ML updates charges an order of magnitude faster than CR-MC while accurately reproducing its equilibrium properties. This framework opens a path toward large-scale simulations of charge-regulating materials for understanding and designing their responsive behavior.

非晶锂三价金属氯化物固态电解质的计算筛选#

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：solid-state electrolytes、molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

非晶固态电解质（SSE）是安全、高能量密度全固态电池的有吸引力的候选者。最近发现非晶态 LiTaCl6 和 LiNbCl6 的室温离子电导率高于 10 mS cm-1，活化能低于 0.2 eV，这激发了人们对新型非晶态卤化物锂导体的广泛研究。在这里，我们报告了利用通用机器学习力场驱动的分子动力学对非晶态锂三价金属氯化物 (Li-M(III)-Cl) 进行的计算筛选。对于涵盖 15 种稀土元素和铟的 16 种 Li-M(III)-Cl 组合物，所有相均表现出快速的室温 Li⁺ 扩散，扩散率约为 2-5 × 10⁻7 cm2 s⁻1。将电导率与扩散率关系缩放至实验 LiTaCl6 参考后，估计的室温电导率范围从非晶态 Li3InCl6 的 7.7 mS cm-1 到非晶态 Li3ScCl6 的 14 mS cm-1。非晶 Li₃ScCl₆ 的活化能为 0.18 eV，预计将成为导电性最强的非晶卤化物电解质之一。为了合理化依赖于成分的输运，我们确定了两个描述符：Li⁺振动态密度（VDOS）中心，它与扩散率成反比，以及弦状Li⁺运动中相关物体的平均数量。其中Li⁺具有较低VDOS中心并且通过单独跳跃而不是通过协同运动迁移更多的组合物表现出较高的锂离子扩散率。这些结果表明非晶卤化物 SSE 具有丰富的锂离子传输特性。

English Abstract

Amorphous solid-state electrolytes (SSEs) are attractive candidates for safe, high-energy-density all-solid-state batteries. The recent discovery that amorphous LiTaCl₆ and LiNbCl₆ reach room-temperature ionic conductivities above 10 mS cm⁻¹ with activation energies below 0.2 eV has spurred a broad search for new amorphous halide Li conductors. Here we report a computational screening of amorphous lithium trivalent-metal chlorides (Li-M(III)-Cl) using molecular dynamics driven by a universal machine-learning force field. For the sixteen Li-M(III)-Cl compositions spanning fifteen rare-earth elements and indium, all phases exhibit fast room-temperature Li⁺ diffusion, with diffusivities of roughly 2-5 × 10⁻⁷ cm² s⁻¹. After scaling the conductivity versus diffusivity relation to an experimental LiTaCl₆ reference, the estimated room-temperature conductivities range from 7.7 mS cm⁻¹ for amorphous Li₃InCl₆ to 14 mS cm⁻¹ for amorphous Li₃ScCl₆. With a low activation energy of 0.18 eV, amorphous Li₃ScCl₆ is predicted to be among the most conductive amorphous halide electrolytes. To rationalize the composition-dependent transport, we identify two descriptors: the Li⁺ vibrational density of states (VDOS) center, which correlates inversely with diffusivity, and the average number of correlated bodies in string-like Li⁺ motion. Compositions in which Li⁺ has a lower VDOS center and migrates more by individual hopping rather than by concerted motion exhibit higher Li-ion diffusivities. These results suggest rich Li-ion transport characteristics in amorphous halide SSEs.

针对非平衡缺陷动力学和塑性的预训练铝 MACE 电位的保留约束微调#

原题：Retention-constrained fine-tuning of a pre-trained aluminium MACE potential for non-equilibrium defect dynamics and plasticity

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory、interatomic potential、molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

针对非平衡缺陷动力学和塑性调整预先训练的机器学习原子间势需要在不降低近平衡弹性和振动响应的情况下提高缺陷精度。在这里，我们通过集成条件读数不确定性、迭代全参数更新和协议兼容的相对能量锚定来实现铝 MACE 势 QP17 的平衡。超过 144 个线性读出权重的灵敏度指导采集，并具有批内协方差更新和不断发展的神经表示。经过四轮主动学习（32 次获取配置和 400 次梯度更新）后，力不确定性获取将平均开发力误差从 22.43 减少到 21.52 meV/Angstrom，在相同更新预算下优于随机采样 (22.70)、几何多样性选择 (22.46) 和所有 104 个候选标签 (22.71) 的统一训练。所有三个力不确定度运行都满足体机械保留标准：历史相对能量误差从 0.375 meV/atom 减少到 0.207 meV/atom，而谐波声子均方根漂移保持在 0.05 THz 以下。匹配的客观消融表明相对能量锚定抑制谐波漂移。跨平面断层、孪晶界和晶界的样本外评估表明，即使从未拟合维里应力，缺陷能量和偏应力也可转移。闭环分子动力学和密度泛函理论 (MD-DFT) 试验证实，自动接受门控会拦截违反历史弹性群能量或振动容差的更新。这些组件共同建立了一个保留约束的微调协议，用于改善塑性变形可观测值，同时保留测试的整体机械响应。

English Abstract

Adapting a pre-trained machine-learning interatomic potential for non-equilibrium defect dynamics and plasticity requires improving defect accuracy without degrading the near-equilibrium elastic and vibrational response. Here we achieve this balance for the aluminium MACE potential QP17 by integrating conditional readout uncertainty, iterative full-parameter updates and protocol-compatible relative-energy anchoring. Sensitivities over 144 linear readout weights guide acquisition, with within-batch covariance updates and evolving neural representations. After four active-learning rounds (32 acquired configurations and 400 gradient updates), force-uncertainty acquisition reduces mean development force error from 22.43 to 21.52 meV/Angstrom, outperforming random sampling (22.70), geometry-diversity selection (22.46) and uniform training across all 104 candidate labels (22.71) under the same update budget. All three force-uncertainty runs satisfy bulk-mechanical retention criteria: historical relative-energy error decreases from 0.375 to 0.207 meV/atom, while harmonic phonon root-mean-square drift remains below 0.05 THz. Matched objective ablations reveal that relative-energy anchoring suppresses harmonic drift. Out-of-sample evaluations across planar faults, twin boundaries and grain boundaries demonstrate transferable gains in defect energies and deviatoric stresses, even though virial stresses are never fitted. A closed-loop molecular dynamics and density functional theory (MD-DFT) pilot confirms that automated acceptance gating intercepts updates that violate historical elastic-group energy or vibrational tolerances. Together, these components establish a retention-constrained fine-tuning protocol for improving plastic-deformation observables while preserving the tested bulk mechanical response.

原题：TCSP3.0: Template-Based Crystal Structure Prediction with Coefficient-Preserving Element Substitution and Structural Diversity
作者：Ying Feng、Jeffrey Hu、Lai Wei、Liang Tao、Madeline Miklos、Dipannoy Das Gupta 等 8 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-27

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：crystal structure prediction。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

基于模板的晶体结构预测（CSP）通过元素替换将已知的晶体结构转移到新的成分，因此其准确性取决于检索合适的模板、映射元素并对所得候选正确排序。在 T180 基准上审核原始 TCSP2.0 实现，我们发现 2,347 个选定结构中有 469 个 (20%) 具有错误的组成，这是由忽略化学计量系数的元素映射以及级联或破坏元素符号的顺序文本替换引起的。我们提出 TCSP3.0，它强制保留系数映射，在解析的结构上同时替换物种并验证每个候选的组成和细胞。它还添加了周期数检索、软氧化态证据、半径兼容性评分、结构重复数据删除以及 ORB-v3 机器学习潜力松弛和其候选者的能量排名，如深层生成 CSP 基线所使用。 TCSP3.0 在 T180 上实现了 72.2% 和 90.6% 的 Top-1 和 Top-20 匹配率，而 TCSP2.0 的匹配率为 28.3% 和 45.6%，在新的 800 目标基准 (T800) 上实现了 74.0% 的 Top-20 匹配率。在 T180 上，它的性能也优于 PNcsp+ 和深度生成模型 CrystalFlow 和 MaskGXT（Top-1 72.2% vs 最多 63.9%）。即使不放松，TCSP3.0 在两个 CPU 内核上每个目标的中位数为 28 秒时，在 Top-1 中达到 67.8%，在 Top-20 中达到 90.6%，大约比 CrystalFlow 快十倍。 T180 和 T800 上的配对十臂消融表明，大部分改进来自正确性修复；结构去重显着提高了Top-5匹配率，覆盖需要基于嵌入的检索，其余评分特征影响较小或为零。

English Abstract

Template-based crystal structure prediction (CSP) transfers a known crystal structure to a new composition by element substitution, so its accuracy depends on retrieving suitable templates, mapping elements and ranking the resulting candidates correctly. Auditing the original TCSP2.0 implementation on the T180 benchmark, we found that 469 of its 2,347 selected structures (20%) have the wrong composition, caused by element mappings that ignore stoichiometric coefficients and by sequential text substitution that cascades or corrupts element symbols. We propose TCSP3.0, which enforces coefficient-preserving mappings, substitutes species simultaneously on parsed structures and validates every candidate's composition and cell. It further adds periodic-number retrieval, soft oxidation-state evidence, radius-compatibility scoring, structural deduplication, and ORB-v3 machine-learning-potential relaxation and energy ranking of its candidates, as used by deep generative CSP baselines. TCSP3.0 achieves Top-1 and Top-20 match rates of 72.2% and 90.6% on T180, compared with 28.3% and 45.6% for TCSP2.0, and a Top-20 match rate of 74.0% on a new 800-target benchmark (T800). On T180 it also outperforms PNcsp+ and deep generative models CrystalFlow and MaskGXT (Top-1 72.2% versus at most 63.9%). Even without relaxation, TCSP3.0 reaches 67.8% at Top-1 and 90.6% at Top-20 in a median of 28 s per target on two CPU cores, about ten times faster than CrystalFlow. Paired ten-arm ablations on T180 and T800 show that most of the improvement comes from the correctness fixes; structural deduplication significantly improves the Top-5 match rate, embedding-based retrieval is required for coverage, and the remaining scoring features have small or null effects.

mdRNAcat：REST2 构象集合的分子动力学 RNA 目录#

原题：mdRNAcat: A Molecular Dynamics RNA Catalog of REST2 Conformational Ensembles

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

学习 RNA 分子模型需要模拟数据来捕获构象变异性并提供一致的身体训练信号。在这里，我们介绍 mdRNAcat，这是一个精选的全原子分子动力学 (MD) 模拟数据集，对 48 个 RNA 系统进行了模拟，旨在促进序列多样性。每个系统均使用具有溶质缩放的副本交换 (REST2) 进行模拟，具有 32 个哈密顿状态，每个状态的产生长度为 200-300 ns。该数据集提供原子坐标、瞬时力和在未缩放参考哈密顿量下重新评估的力，以及结构注释和副本交换元数据。参考状态的坐标和力的间隔为 1 ps，所有状态的坐标和力的间隔为 100 ps，采用标准化 HDF5 格式。我们描述了构象采样、交换行为、碱基配对和糖褶分布，以记录模拟集合的属性。 mdRNAcat 提供了可重复使用的资源，用于开发和评估数据驱动的 RNA 模型，包括粗粒度力场。

English Abstract

Learning molecular models of RNA requires simulation data that capture conformational variability and provide consistent physical training signals. Here, we introduce mdRNAcat, a curated dataset of all-atom molecular dynamics (MD) simulations of 48 RNA systems selected to promote sequence diversity. Each system is simulated using replica-exchange with solute scaling (REST2), with 32 Hamiltonian states and production lengths of 200-300 ns per state. The dataset provides atomic coordinates, instantaneous forces, and forces reevaluated under the unscaled reference Hamiltonian, together with structural annotations and replica-exchange metadata. Coordinates and forces are available at 1 ps intervals for the reference state and 100 ps intervals across all states in a standardized HDF5 format. We characterize conformational sampling, exchange behavior, base pairing, and sugar-pucker distributions to document the properties of the simulated ensembles. mdRNAcat provides a reusable resource for developing and evaluating data-driven RNA models, including coarse-grained force fields.

用于原子能量预测的电荷密度监督实空间表示#

原题：Charge density-supervised real-space representations for atomistic energy prediction

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：graph neural network。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

基态电子密度包含足够的信息来确定基态能量，同时提供空间电子信息。这项研究开发了一种电荷密度信息图神经网络（以下简称 CDGNN），它从等变原子图中联合学习能量和价电子密度。这种方法将密度监督的真实空间探针表示合并到全局能量回归器中，允许与密度相关的空间信息有助于能量预测。在 QM9 数据集上，CDGNN 相对于等变 GNN 基线减少了能量预测误差，用于对较大分子进行插值和外推。对于小型含 H Fe 和 Fe-C 数据集，CDGNN 的表现与基线类似，表明密度监督具有与数据集相关的优势。 CDGNN 还准确预测了分子和铁基系统的电荷密度分布。注意力分析显示，铁基结构中氢原子附近的探针表示权重较高。这些发现凸显了整合电荷密度信息进行能量预测的潜力及其与任务相关的好处。

English Abstract

Ground-state electron density contains sufficient information to determine ground-state energy, while providing spatial electronic information. This study developed a charge-density-informed graph neural network (hereafter CDGNN) that jointly learns energy and valence electron density from equivariant atomic graphs. This approach incorporates density-supervised real-space probe representations into the global energy regressor, allowing density-related spatial information to contribute to energy prediction. On the QM9 dataset, CDGNN reduced the energy prediction errors relative to an equivariant GNN baseline for interpolation and extrapolation to larger molecules. For small H-containing Fe and Fe-C datasets, CDGNN performed similarly to the baseline, indicating the dataset-dependent benefits of density supervision. CDGNN also accurately predicted the charge density distributions for molecular and Fe-based systems. The attention analysis showed higher weights for probe representations near the H atoms in Fe-based structures. These findings highlight the potential of integrating charge-density information for energy prediction and its task-dependent benefits.

化学反应网络的生成发现#

原题：Generative discovery of chemical reaction networks
作者：Yunhong Han、Gaopeng Ren、Yan Liu、Ronghua Zhang、Huangjiang Huang、Ruiqi Zhang 等 19 人。arXiv · physics，2026-10-30

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory、interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

化学动力学决定分子稳定性、反应选择性和反应混合物的演变，但发现潜在的反应网络仍然具有挑战性。在这里，我们介绍 ReactNexus，这是一个仅从反应物图开始的动力学引导网络探索的生成框架。生成模型提出过渡态并预测连接反应物和产物的完整反应轨迹，同时使用机器学习的原子间势进行计算验证连接性并评估电子势垒。在 10,000 个生成的候选物的分布基准上，经过密度泛函理论 (DFT) 优化和内在反应坐标验证后，80.8% 连接到输入反应物。由此产生的网络揭示了较低障碍的合作途径以及催化剂释放和催化剂结合分支之间的竞争。在所检查的碳正离子级联中，已知产物前体的覆盖范围在有限扩展后饱和，并且不同的条目汇聚到共享中间体上，表明紧凑的可访问反应空间。网络衍生的候选结构进一步协助复杂亲核芳香取代反应的质谱解释。这些结果共同建立了一种反应网络发现和机理分析的生成方法，无需预定义的产品或反应模板。

English Abstract

Chemical kinetics determines molecular stability, reaction selectivity and the evolution of reacting mixtures, but discovering the underlying reaction networks remains challenging. Here we introduce ReactNexus, a generative framework for kinetic-guided network exploration starting solely from reactant graphs. Generative models propose transition states and predict the full reaction trajectories connecting reactants and products, while calculations using a machine-learned interatomic potential verify connectivity and evaluate electronic barriers. On an in-distribution benchmark of 10,000 generated candidates, 80.8% connect to the input reactant after density functional theory (DFT) optimization and intrinsic reaction coordinate verification. The resulting networks uncover lower-barrier cooperative pathways and competition between catalyst release and catalyst-bound branches. In the examined carbocation cascades, coverage of known product precursors saturates after limited expansion and distinct entries converge onto shared intermediates, indicating a compact accessible reaction space. Network-derived candidate structures further assist mass-spectral interpretation of a complex nucleophilic aromatic substitution reaction. Together, these results establish a generative approach to reaction-network discovery and mechanistic analysis without predefined products or reaction templates.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：monte carlo simulation。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

垂直排列的 MoS2 薄膜与 Cu 顶部电极配对，已被用作电化学金属化电池忆阻器器件。忆阻开关被归因于导电丝的形成，但它们的确切结构仍然难以捉摸。在这里，我们对铜嵌入、聚类和场驱动动力学进行了多尺度计算研究，以最终模拟操作条件下的细丝形成。我们使用密度泛函理论结果来微调机器学习力场，并且最重要的物理现象被进一步提炼为多粒子动力学蒙特卡罗模拟。通过对 Cu 插层结构的详尽列举，我们发现 MoS2Cu0.11 成分中最稳定的结构，其中 Cu 原子在 MoS2 层之间形成骰子晶格。这些结构显示出金属特性，因此可以在忆阻器操作下形成导电丝。模拟的开关速度和阈值电压与可用的实验数据非常吻合。我们的研究结果为器件运行机制提供了基本见解，并为最大限度地提高开关速度和耐用性提供了指导。

English Abstract

Vertically-aligned MoS₂ films paired with a Cu top electrode have been used as electrochemical metallization cell memristor devices. The memristive switching has been attributed to the formation of conductive filaments, yet their exact structure has remained elusive. Here we carry out a multi-scale computational study of Cu intercalation, clustering, and field-driven dynamics to eventually model the filament formation under operating conditions. We use density-functional theory results to fine-tune a machine learning force field, and the most important physical phenomena are further distilled into multi-particle kinetic Monte Carlo simulations. By exhaustive enumeration of Cu intercalated structures, we find the most stable structure at MoS₂Cu₀.₁₁ composition, with Cu atoms forming a dice lattice between the MoS₂ layers. These structures are shown to exhibit metallic character and can thus form conductive filaments under memristor operation. The simulated switching speed and threshold voltage agree well with the available experimental data. Our results give fundamental insights on the device operation mechanism and guidelines for maximizing switching speed and endurance.

错误所在：学习型 Kohn-Sham 哈密顿量的谱表示#

原题：Where the Error Lives: Spectral Representations of Learned Kohn-Sham Hamiltonians

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory、electronic structure。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

Kohn-Sham 密度泛函理论 (DFT) 是计算分子和材料电子结构的标准方法，但其自洽场 (SCF) 迭代使其对于大型系统和高通量筛选来说成本高昂。机器学习哈密顿模型提供了一条捷径：等变网络在一次前向传递中预测 Kohn-Sham 哈密顿矩阵，并对预测进行对角化产生轨道能量、HOMO-LUMO 能隙、分子轨道和电子密度。这些模型经过训练以逐个匹配参考矩阵条目，但较小的条目误差并不能保证准确的特征值或特征向量。两种基于物理的替代方案半途而废：最近提出的波函数对准损失增加了光谱惩罚，而不对预测进行对角化，并且匹配对角化预测的电子密度直接对其进行监督。两者都不完整：通过在参考哈密顿量的本征基中表示预测误差，我们表明每个都只看到部分误差，并且移动预测的轨道能量使电子密度完全不变。相同的分析确定了完整的训练信号：累积局部态密度（累积 LDOS），即空间中每个点能量低于给定阈值的电子数量。累积 LDOS 是一个单一的物理可观测量，其中包含作为特殊情况的电子密度，响应误差的每个分量，唯一地确定哈密顿量，并产生不会因大轨道能量误差而消失的梯度。以这种方式微调 QH9 上的三个哈密顿网络（我们称之为 FermiScope），可以在不增加元素误差的情况下减少 HOMO-LUMO 间隙误差。这些增益会延续到分布外和分子动力学分裂中，而波函数对准损失的帮助较小。

English Abstract

Kohn-Sham density functional theory (DFT) is the standard method for computing the electronic structure of molecules and materials, but its self-consistent-field (SCF) iterations make it expensive for large systems and high-throughput screening. Machine-learned Hamiltonian models offer a shortcut: an equivariant network predicts the Kohn-Sham Hamiltonian matrix in one forward pass, and diagonalizing the prediction yields orbital energies, the HOMO-LUMO gap, molecular orbitals and the electron density. These models are trained to match the reference matrix entry by entry, yet a small entry-wise error does not guarantee accurate eigenvalues or eigenvectors. Two physics-informed alternatives go partway: a recently proposed wavefunction-alignment loss adds a spectral penalty without diagonalizing the prediction, and matching the electron density of the diagonalized prediction supervises it directly. Neither is complete: by representing the prediction error in the eigenbasis of the reference Hamiltonian, we show that each sees only part of the error, and that shifting the predicted orbital energies leaves the electron density exactly unchanged. The same analysis identifies a complete training signal: the cumulative local density of states (cumulative LDOS), the number of electrons at each point in space with energy below a given threshold. The cumulative LDOS is a single physical observable that contains the electron density as a special case, responds to every component of the error, determines the Hamiltonian uniquely, and yields gradients that do not vanish for large orbitalenergy errors. Fine-tuning three Hamiltonian networks on QH9 in this way, which we call FermiScope, reduces the HOMO-LUMO gap error without increasing the element-wise error. The gains carry over to out-of-distribution and molecular-dynamics splits, where the wavefunction-alignment loss helps less.

框架条件开放材料生成#

原题：Framework-Conditional Open Materials Generation
作者：Maya M. Martirossyan、Hillary Pan、Philipp Höllmer、Stefano Martiniani。arXiv · cond-mat.supr-con，2026-10-30

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

新型材料的理论预测依赖于多种技术，从基于数据挖掘的离子替代方法到完全从头生成模型。每种方法都会施加特定的限制，并在超越已知材料的分布来推断新材料时遇到独特的挑战。材料生成的首要目标是生产稳定、独特和新颖的 (S.U.N.) 结构。然而，满足这些标准的预测结构通常是由映射到已知结构原型或框架上的新颖化学成分产生的。我们利用这种启发式见解提出了一种新的生成范式，用于从头预测受其结构框架（即晶胞形状和各自的颗粒位置）约束的无机晶体材料。我们的方法称为 OMatG-FC（开放材料框架-条件生成），通过结合原子种类的离散流匹配和用于单位晶格体积缩放的随机插值来进行学习，同时固定分数坐标和单位体积晶格向量，与无约束（无框架）生成相比，大大降低了问题的维度。我们的方法比现有的原型约束方法更灵活，展示了从已知结构框架中发现看不见的稳定原型的能力。我们还调查了 MP-20 数据集中独特框架的数量，展示了数据集中重复框架的倾向，并提供了框架分割版本。我们使用 LeMat-GenBench 套件使用三个 ML 原子间势对模型的性能进行基准测试，并实现了最先进的 S.U.N.速率低于凸包，避免了为了材料发现而学习原子堆积的需要。

English Abstract

Theoretical predictions of novel materials rely on a variety of techniques, from data-mining-based ion substitution methods to fully de novo generative models. Each approach imposes specific constraints and encounters unique challenges when extrapolating beyond the distribution of known materials to discover new ones. The foremost goal of materials generation is to produce stable, unique, and novel (S.U.N.) structures. Predicted structures that meet these criteria, however, typically result from novel chemical compositions mapped onto known structural prototypes or frameworks. We leverage this heuristic insight to present a new generative paradigm for the de novo prediction of inorganic crystalline materials constrained by their structural framework---i.e., the unit cell shapes and respective particle positions. Our method, termed OMatG-FC (Open Materials Framework-Conditional Generation), learns via a combination of discrete flow matching on the atomic species and stochastic interpolants for the volume scaling of the unit cell lattice, while fixing in-place the fractional coordinates and the unit-volume lattice vectors, dramatically reducing the dimensionality of the problem compared to unconstrained (framework-free) generation. Our method is more flexible than existing prototype-constrained methods, demonstrating capability for discovery of unseen, stable prototypes derived from known structural frameworks. We also investigate the number of unique frameworks in the MP-20 dataset, demonstrating the propensity of duplicate frameworks within the dataset and providing a framework-split version. We benchmark the performance of our model using the LeMat-GenBench suite using three ML interatomic potentials and achieve state-of-the-art S.U.N. rate below the convex hull, eschewing the need to learn atomic packings for materials discovery.

AtomMOF：用于 MOF 吸附结构预测的全原子流匹配#

原题：AtomMOF: All-Atom Flow Matching for MOF-Adsorbate Structure Prediction
作者：Nayoung Kim、Honghui Kim、Sihyun Yu、Minkyu Kim、Seongsu Kim、Sungsoo Ahn。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-26

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：金属有机框架（MOF）是用于直接空气捕获等应用的有前景的材料，其性能取决于吸附物在框架内的结合方式。因此，准确预测 MOF 吸附物构型对于筛选候选材料非常重要。然而，传统方法的计算成本很高，并且需要已知的主体结构，而现有的生成模型依赖于刚体假设，并且没有明确地模拟吸附物。我们引入了 AtomMOF，这是一种可扩展的全原子流匹配模型，可以从离散的构建块和吸附物中联合预测 MOF 吸附物结构。 AtomMOF 建立在具有基于构建块的成对注意…

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

金属有机框架（MOF）是用于直接空气捕获等应用的有前景的材料，其性能取决于吸附物在框架内的结合方式。因此，准确预测 MOF 吸附物构型对于筛选候选材料非常重要。然而，传统方法的计算成本很高，并且需要已知的主体结构，而现有的生成模型依赖于刚体假设，并且没有明确地模拟吸附物。我们引入了 AtomMOF，这是一种可扩展的全原子流匹配模型，可以从离散的构建块和吸附物中联合预测 MOF 吸附物结构。 AtomMOF 建立在具有基于构建块的成对注意力偏差的扩散变压器之上，在不受约束的全原子空间中运行，并表现出清晰的缩放行为。为了提高灵活全原子模型的结构有效性，我们还提出了具有机器学习原子间势（MLIP）的 Feynman-Kac（FK）转向。在 BW 数据集上，与之前的工作相比，AtomMOF 的匹配率相对提高了 58.17%，RMSD 相对降低了 31.84%。 MLIP引导的转向进一步将有效性提高了28.7%，并将地层能量误差降低了86.5%。在 ODAC25 上，AtomMOF 比 GCMC 更快地生成吸附构型，并且与 MLIP 弛豫结合使用时，可识别比参考数据集中的能量更低的构型。

English Abstract

Metal-organic frameworks (MOFs) are promising materials for applications such as direct air capture, where performance depends on how adsorbates bind within the framework. Accurately predicting MOF-adsorbate configurations is therefore important for screening candidate materials. However, traditional approaches are computationally expensive and require a known host structure, while existing generative models rely on rigid-body assumptions and do not explicitly model adsorbates. We introduce AtomMOF, a scalable all-atom flow-matching model that jointly predicts MOF-adsorbate structures from discrete building blocks and adsorbates. Built on a Diffusion Transformer with a building block-based pairwise attention bias, AtomMOF operates in an unconstrained all-atom space and exhibits clear scaling behavior. To improve the structural validity of flexible all-atom models, we also propose Feynman-Kac (FK) steering with machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs). On the BW dataset, AtomMOF achieves a 58.17% relative increase in match rate and a 31.84% relative reduction in RMSD compared with prior work. MLIP-guided steering further improves validity by 28.7% and reduces formation energy error by 86.5%. On ODAC25, AtomMOF generates adsorption configurations faster than GCMC and, when combined with MLIP relaxation, identifies lower-energy configurations than those in the reference dataset.

结合模拟和光谱学设计硫化物固体电解质的保护涂层#

原题：Combining Simulations and Spectroscopy to Design Protective Coatings on Sulfide Solid Electrolytes

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

硫化物固体电解质由于其高锂离子电导率和良好的加工性能而对全固态电池有吸引力，但其实际应用受到电化学不稳定性和湿气引起的降解的限制。分子表面涂层提供了一种可扩展的策略来提高耐湿性和界面稳定性，但对控制表面相互作用的分子水平因素的了解有限。通过将机器学习潜力实现的大规模原子模拟与目标实验相结合，我们提出了一个用于设计 Li₆PS₅Cl (LPSCl) 分子涂层的综合框架。使用在涵盖 LPSCl 表面、分子吸附物及其相互作用的 r²SCAN 参考数据集上微调的 PET-MAD 原子间势，我们模拟了具有不同长度烷基链的羧酸 (CA)、磺酸 (SA) 和膦酸 (PA) 的表面吸附以及这些涂层表面与水在有限温度下的相互作用。模拟表明，锂化头基通常表现出更有利的吸附能。阻抗谱表明，粉末加工后，锂化涂层仍保持初始离子电导率的40--55%。虽然吸附强度主要由头基化学和锂化状态控制，但涂层形态主要取决于烷基链长度和表面覆盖率。特别是，长链锂化羧酸盐形成致密的刷状疏水层，减少水进入活性锂表面位点，并在涂层厚度约为 0.8 nm 时提供保护。与这些预测一致，较长的羧酸盐链可以更有效地抑制 H2S 的释放。由于较厚的涂层也会阻碍锂离子传输，因此中等链长可在防潮和离子电导率之间提供最佳平衡。

English Abstract

Sulfide solid electrolytes are attractive for all-solid-state batteries because of their high Li-ion conductivity and favorable processability, but their practical use is limited by electrochemical instability and moisture-induced degradation. Molecular surface coatings offer a scalable strategy to improve humidity tolerance and interfacial stability, yet there is limited understanding of the molecular-level factors governing surface interactions. By combining large-scale atomistic simulations enabled by a machine-learning potential with targeted experiments, we present a comprehensive framework for designing molecular coatings on Li₆PS₅Cl (LPSCl). Using a PET-MAD interatomic potential fine-tuned on an r²SCAN reference dataset covering LPSCl surfaces, molecular adsorbates, and their interactions, we model surface adsorption of carboxylic (CA), sulfonic (SA), and phosphonic (PA) acids with alkyl chains of varying lengths and the interaction of these coated surfaces with water at finite temperature. The simulations show that lithiated head groups generally exhibit more favorable adsorption energies. Impedance spectroscopy shows that the lithiated coatings maintain 40--55% of the initial ionic conductivity after powder processing. While adsorption strength is largely controlled by head-group chemistry and lithiation state, coating morphology depends primarily on alkyl-chain length and surface coverage. In particular, long-chain lithiated carboxylates form dense, brush-like hydrophobic layers that reduce water access to reactive Li surface sites, with protection setting in at a coating thickness of approximately 0.8 nm. Consistent with these predictions, longer carboxylate chains suppress H₂S evolution more effectively. Because thicker coatings also hinder Li-ion transport, intermediate chain lengths offer the best balance between moisture protection and ionic conductivity.

MatOS ：一种用于材料研究的代理操作系统#

作者：Dingyang Lyu、Jing Gao、Zhonghao Chang、Jingjie Huang、Xiangran Cheng、Xin He 等 16 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-27

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：材料研究依赖于分散且不断增长的数据、知识和模拟工具，一项研究通常跨越文献分析、计算和实验等多天的工作流程。大型语言模型代理现在可以检索材料数据，操作模拟代码，并与自主实验室关闭设计-合成-表征循环。然而，用新功能扩展这些代理、让长时间运行的任务保持在正轨上并让它们从过去的任务和用户反馈中学习仍然很困难。在这里，我们介绍 MatOS，一个用于材料研究的代理操作系统，具有三个核心组件：（1）分层科学资产图，将数据、知识、工具、技能、角色和工…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

材料研究依赖于分散且不断增长的数据、知识和模拟工具，一项研究通常跨越文献分析、计算和实验等多天的工作流程。大型语言模型代理现在可以检索材料数据，操作模拟代码，并与自主实验室关闭设计-合成-表征循环。然而，用新功能扩展这些代理、让长时间运行的任务保持在正轨上并让它们从过去的任务和用户反馈中学习仍然很困难。在这里，我们介绍 MatOS，一个用于材料研究的代理操作系统，具有三个核心组件：（1）分层科学资产图，将数据、知识、工具、技能、角色和工作流程组织为可重用资产； （2）有状态的自适应工作流程，将任务作为具有科学检查的显式执行图来运行，以便可以恢复和跟踪它们； (3) 证据驱动的资产演变，根据执行记录和用户反馈修改这些资产。在 ResearchClawBench 上，MatOS 使用相同的基础模型比之前表现最佳的智能体提高了 11.15%，并扩展了成本分数 Pareto 前沿。凭借通用的基础模型，它的性能优于 Codex CLI 和 Claude Code，在 MatTools 上超过 Codex CLI 2.13%，在 SciAgentGym 上超过 Codex CLI 4.17%。在 8 个研究案例中，MatOS 将超过 3000 万个候选结构缩小为 97 个固体电解质候选，识别了 89 个卤化物变体，其计算电导率高于其母体结构，将 11 个预训练原子间势的力误差与局部原子环境联系起来，并支持两种新方法，相对于其基线，误差分别减少了 3.1% 和 13.6%。这些结果表明，将研究能力组织为可重复使用、不断发展的资产，使代理商能够进行长期、多阶段的材料研究。代码可在 https://github.com/DingyangLyu/MatOS 获取。

English Abstract

Materials research relies on scattered and growing data, knowledge, and simulation tools, and a single study often spans multi-day workflows across literature analysis, computation, and experiment. Large language model agents can now retrieve materials data, operate simulation codes, and close design-synthesis-characterization loops with autonomous laboratories. However, extending these agents with new capabilities, keeping long-running tasks on track, and letting them learn from past tasks and user feedback remain difficult. Here we present MatOS, an agent operating system for materials research with three core components: (1) a layered scientific asset graph that organizes data, knowledge, tools, skills, roles, and workflows as reusable assets; (2) a stateful adaptive workflow that runs tasks as explicit execution graphs with scientific checks, so that they can be resumed and traced; and (3) evidence-driven asset evolution, which revises these assets from execution records and user feedback. On ResearchClawBench, MatOS improves on the previously best-performing agent by 11.15% with the same base model and extends the cost-score Pareto frontier. With a common base model, it outperforms Codex CLI and Claude Code, exceeding Codex CLI by 2.13% on MatTools and 4.17% on SciAgentGym. In eight research cases, MatOS narrowed more than 30 million candidate structures to 97 solid-electrolyte candidates, identified 89 halide variants with higher computed conductivity than their parent structures, linked the force errors of 11 pretrained interatomic potentials to local atomic environments, and supported two new methods that reduce errors by 3.1% and 13.6% relative to their baselines. These results show that organizing research capabilities as reusable, evolving assets enables agents to carry out long, multistage materials studies. Code is available at https://github.com/DingyangLyu/MatOS.

平面能量学不能决定纳米晶铝中的孪生持久性#

原题：Planar energetics do not determine twin persistence in nanocrystalline aluminium

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

平面能量学是面心立方金属孪生性的标准描述符，也是原子间势的常用验证目标。在这里，我们表明他们不能确定变形孪晶是否存在于纳米晶铝中。两个机器学习势再现相同的零负载滑移曲线和紧密间隔的层状能量表面，根据相同负载下的常见分离断层前兆，预测保留的孪生覆盖率分别为 64% 和 14%。这种分歧归因于扭曲的部分核心内沿着尾部平移作用的力，这将消除外部断层。平面能量学确实设定了界限：外部断层超过了中面剪切增量障碍的一半以上，但孪生完井和断层去除仍然仅相距 10--21 mJ mtextsuperscript-2。组合孪生体是否持续存在取决于局部牵引力相对于全滑移平分线的方向以及来自晶界网络的部分滑移前沿的到达顺序。

English Abstract

Planar energetics are the standard descriptors of twinnability in face-centred cubic metals and the usual validation targets for interatomic potentials. Here we show that they do not determine whether deformation twins persist in nanocrystalline aluminium. Two machine-learned potentials reproducing the same zero-load slip curves and closely spaced layered energy surfaces predict, from a common separated-fault precursor under identical loading, retained twin coverage of 64% and 14%. The divergence traces to forces within distorted partial cores acting along the trailing translation that would remove an outer fault. Planar energetics do set the margin: an outer fault more than halves the incremental barrier for middle-plane shear, yet twin completion and fault removal remain separated by only 10--21 mJ mtextsuperscript-2. Whether an assembled twin persists is decided instead by the orientation of local traction relative to the full-slip bisector and by the arrival order of partial-slip fronts from the grain-boundary network.

Crystoria：连接材料 AI 热力学和动态稳定性的大型晶体数据集#

原题：Crystoria: A Large-Scale Crystal Dataset Linking Thermodynamic and Dynamical Stability for Materials AI
作者：Ke-Xin Pang、Ze-Feng Gao、Yan Gao、Zhong-Yi Lu。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-10-30
🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉6方向匹配3

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：大规模晶体数据库使材料信息学能够获取数以百万计的结构和热力学性质，从而改变了材料的发现。然而，动态稳定性在可比较的规模上仍然很少被表征，因为它的评估比传统的结构和能量计算对计算的要求更高。这种不匹配在快速膨胀的晶体结构空间与可用于材料研究和机器学习的物理信息之间造成了严重的差距。在这里，我们介绍 Crystoria，一个大型晶体数据集，旨在为现代材料数据规模带来动态稳定性。我们通过系统地构建低能和亚稳态无机晶体，标准化其结构特性，并将机…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

大规模晶体数据库使材料信息学能够获取数以百万计的结构和热力学性质，从而改变了材料的发现。然而，动态稳定性在可比较的规模上仍然很少被表征，因为它的评估比传统的结构和能量计算对计算的要求更高。这种不匹配在快速膨胀的晶体结构空间与可用于材料研究和机器学习的物理信息之间造成了严重的差距。在这里，我们介绍 Crystoria，一个大型晶体数据集，旨在为现代材料数据规模带来动态稳定性。我们通过系统地构建低能和亚稳态无机晶体，标准化其结构特性，并将机器学习加速的结构弛豫与基于声子的动态筛选相结合来构建 Crystoria。生成的数据集包含 2,649,640 个晶体结构，涵盖 89 个元素、230 个空间群中的 219 个，以及包含 1-48 个原子的原始晶胞。其中，1,793,353 个结构（67.68%）被归类为动态稳定结构，揭示了化学和晶体空间动态稳定性的显着变化。通过为数百万个晶体结构带来动态稳定性，Crystoria 为材料人工智能 (AI) 提供了物理基础，并能够系统地探索动态晶体景观。

English Abstract

Large-scale crystal databases have transformed materials discovery by making millions of structures and thermodynamic properties accessible to materials informatics. Yet dynamical stability remains sparsely characterized at a comparable scale because its evaluation is substantially more computationally demanding than conventional structure and energy calculations. This mismatch creates a critical gap between the rapidly expanding crystal-structure space and the physical information available for materials research and machine learning. Here we introduce Crystoria, a large-scale crystal dataset designed to bring dynamical stability to the scale of modern materials data. We construct Crystoria by systematically curating low-energy and metastable inorganic crystals, standardizing their structural identities, and combining machine-learning-accelerated structural relaxation with phonon-based dynamical screening. The resulting dataset contains 2,649,640 crystal structures, spanning 89 elements, 219 of the 230 space groups, and primitive cells containing 1-48 atoms. Among them, 1,793,353 structures (67.68%) are classified as dynamically stable, revealing substantial variation in dynamical stability across chemical and crystallographic space. By bringing dynamical stability to the scale of millions of crystal structures, Crystoria provides a physically grounded foundation for materials artificial intelligence (AI) and enables systematic exploration of the dynamical crystal landscape.
